                "case_cloud_id": "case-1",
                "case_review": {"style": "自然"},
                "level": "L2",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_truth_revision_zero_falls_back_to_immutable_import(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-v0.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _structured_item(db)

            truth = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)

            assert truth.source == "baseline_import"
            assert truth.revision == 0
            assert truth.revision_id is None
            assert truth.truth_kind == "structured_fields"
            assert truth.expected_level is None
            assert truth.expected_result == {
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
                "is_single": 0,
                "style": "",
            }
            assert truth.workflow_input == {
                "source": "domestic",
                "is_single": 0,
                "ll_id": "ll-1",
            }
            assert len(truth.canonical_hash) == 64
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_truth_revisions_append_hash_chain_and_replay_idempotently(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-chain.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _structured_item(db)
            original = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            first = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                reason="运营确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )
            replay = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                reason="运营确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )

            assert replay.id == first.id
            assert first.revision == 1
            assert first.previous_hash == original.canonical_hash
            assert len(first.canonical_hash) == 64

            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as conflict:
                append_baseline_truth_revision(
                    db,
                    item=item,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result={
                        "ll_id": "ll-1",
                        "is_single": 0,
                        "style": "工业",
                    },
                    truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                    workflow_input={
                        "source": "domestic",
                        "is_single": 0,
                        "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    },
                    source_baseline_run_id=None,
                    source_production_run_id=None,
                    source_correction_key="correction:kg:1",
                    reason="冲突重放",
                    actor="ops",
                )
            assert conflict.value.code == "truth_revision_idempotency_conflict"

            second = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "自然",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:kg:2",
                reason="运营再次确认风格",
                actor="ops",
            )

            assert second.revision == 2
            assert second.previous_hash == first.canonical_hash
            effective = resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)
            assert effective.source == "revision"
            assert effective.revision == 2
            assert effective.revision_id == second.id
            assert effective.expected_result["style"] == "自然"
            assert effective.canonical_hash == second.canonical_hash
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_truth_revision_rows_cannot_be_updated_or_deleted(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'truth-immutable.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            item = _structured_item(db)
            row = append_baseline_truth_revision(
                db,
                item=item,
                expected_level=None,
                expected_result={
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "style": "现代简约",
                },
                truth_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.domestic.whole.v1",
                workflow_input={
                    "source": "domestic",
                    "is_single": 0,
                    "ll_id": "ll-1",
                },
                source_baseline_run_id=None,
                source_production_run_id=None,
                source_correction_key="correction:immutable:1",
                reason="运营确认",
                actor="ops",
            )
            row_id = row.id
            db.commit()

            row.reason = "不允许覆盖"
            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthFrozenError):
                db.flush()
            db.rollback()

            persisted = db.get(BaselineTruthRevision, row_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert json.loads(persisted.expected_result_json)["style"] == "现代简约"
            db.delete(persisted)
            with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthFrozenError):
                db.flush()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_concurrency_recovery_20260912.py =====
"""Concurrency self-throttling regressions observed on run328 (2026-09-12).

Observed: admission collapsed to 9/14/4 of 3x32 slots because the phase
timer counted executor queue wait; ~18% ServerOverloaded 429s never reached
the ramp's pressure bar; rejected waiters shared one available_at; and a lost
pool lock discarded a fully prepared request body. No provider calls here.
"""
import asyncio
import json
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW  # noqa: F401
from test_batch_control_windows import failure


def success(db, number, seconds):
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='succeeded',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-other', quota_pool_id=1,
        upstream_status_code=200, reserved_input_tokens=30000, reserved_output_tokens=0,
        estimated_cost_micros=1, created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300),
        finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds), evidence_json='{}'))
    db.flush()


def _warm(db, pool, rpm):
    admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
    evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
    # Ramp window already open for 100 s; the next admission at NOW+100 is
    # inside the 180 s idle threshold but past the 180 s ramp window.
    evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(
        requests_per_minute=rpm, tokens_per_minute=rpm*30000,
        ramp_started_at=(NOW-timedelta(seconds=100)).timestamp())
    row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
    db.commit()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('rejections,expected_rpm', [
    (0, 12.0),   # healthy window keeps the 1.2x ramp
    (1, 12.0),
    (3, 12.0),   # 3/13 = 23% ServerOverloaded tracks provider load: reported, not a hold
    # 10/20 = 50% is the same signal at a different share, and on 2026-09-15 the
    # majority rule that used to cut here is what pinned production at 2 RPM: a
    # rejection lands in the window immediately, a success minutes later, so a
    # slow stream always looks like a majority. Only RequestBurstTooFast holds
    # the ramp now; see test_batch_overload_does_not_hold_ramp.
    (10, 12.0),
    (18, 12.0),  # 18/28 = 64%, still the provider's load and still not a hold
])
def test_overload_rate_is_reported_and_never_holds_the_ramp(database, rejections, expected_rpm):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # Older physical requests whose responses land after the warm admission;
        # the controller state must stay on the newest admitted row.
        for index in range(10):
            success(db, 200+index, 20+index)
        for index in range(rejections):
            failure(db, 300+index, 5+index)  # early finishes: their 5/10/20 s backoff has expired
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_rpm)
        assert state['feedback']['resource_rejections'] == rejections
        assert 'hold' not in state['feedback']
        assert state['feedback']['overload_rate'] == pytest.approx(rejections/(10+rejections), abs=1e-4)


def test_burst_protection_at_the_same_share_does_hold_the_ramp(database):
    # The contrast that makes the case above a measurement rather than a shrug.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        for index in range(10):
            success(db, 200+index, 20+index)
        for index in range(10):
            failure(db, 300+index, 5+index, code='RequestBurstTooFast')
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(8.0)
        assert state['feedback']['pressure'] is True


def test_overload_rate_ignored_below_minimum_sample(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        success(db, 200, 20)
        failure(db, 300, 5)  # 1/2 = 50% but only two completions
        db.commit()
        _warm(db, pool, 10)
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))
        assert state['feedback']['overload_rate'] == 0.0
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(12.0)


def test_rejected_waiters_get_spread_retry_inside_one_gap(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)  # 2 RPM cold start: 30 s gap
    seen = set()
    for number in range(2, 22):
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                admit(db, pool, number, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1))
            assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
            # delay 29 s plus 0..15 s jitter (capped by the gap itself)
            assert 29 <= caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 45
            seen.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
    assert len(seen) > 1


def test_phase_peak_excludes_executor_queue_wait():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime

    async def scenario():
        def operation():
            time.sleep(.05)
            return True
            yield  # pragma: no cover - generator form
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=6, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            await asyncio.gather(*(runtime.run(operation()) for _ in range(6)))
            # Six 50 ms phases behind one thread: the last one queues for
            # roughly five phases, so queue wait dwarfs any single phase.
            assert runtime.phase_queue_peak_seconds >= 2*runtime.phase_peak_seconds
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_single_slow_preparation_does_not_halve_admission():
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionPolicy, Sample
    policy = AdmissionPolicy(ceiling=32, initial=32)
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.5)) == 32
    assert policy.reason == 'validated_ceiling'
    # 2026-09-19: one slow sample only holds; the second consecutive one cuts
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 9.5)) == 32
    assert policy.reason == 'phase_watch'
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 9.5)) == 16
    assert policy.reason == 'local_resource_pressure'
    for _ in range(2):
        policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.9))
    assert policy.observe(Sample(100, 20, 30, 6000, 2.9)) > 16


def test_async_consumers_back_off_when_nothing_is_claimable():
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    attempts = []

    async def step():
        attempts.append(time.monotonic())
        return False

    async def scenario():
        end = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()+.3
        await consume(step, slots=1, should_continue=lambda: asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < end,
                      poll_seconds=.02, shutdown_seconds=.1)
    asyncio.run(scenario())
    # Fixed 20 ms polling would attempt ~15 times; backoff caps at 4x poll.
    assert 2 <= len(attempts) <= 8


def test_prepared_hold_only_for_short_submission_waits():
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import _prepared_hold_seconds
    from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError

    def error(code='model_batch_submission_wait', wait=20, retryable=True):
        return WorkflowModelBudgetError(code, 'wait', status_code=409,
                                        retryable=retryable, retry_after_seconds=wait)
    deadline = datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(hours=1)
    kwargs = dict(transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=deadline, held_seconds=0.0)
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), **kwargs) == 20.0
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(wait=90), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(code='quota_pool_cooling_down'), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(retryable=False), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(wait=True), **kwargs) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), **{**kwargs, 'held_seconds': 170.0}) is None
    soon = datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(seconds=40)
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=soon, held_seconds=0.0) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='batch', transport_deadline=None, held_seconds=0.0) is None
    assert _prepared_hold_seconds(error(), transport_mode='online', transport_deadline=None, held_seconds=0.0) == 20.0


def test_large_waiting_population_is_spread_by_waiters_over_rpm(database):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission, WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS
    from app.models import ModelQuotaPool
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)  # 2 RPM: 30 s gap
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        plain, spread = set(), set()
        for _ in range(12):
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1))
            plain.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
                check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1), waiting_hint=253)
            spread.add(caught.value.retry_after_seconds)
        assert max(plain) <= 45                      # delay 29 + jitter <= 15
        assert max(spread) <= 45+WAIT_SPREAD_MAX_SECONDS
        assert max(spread) > 60 and min(spread) >= 29   # 253 waiters at 2 RPM: up to +600 s
        # a single waiter never gets spread
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            check_submission(db, pool=row, reserved_tokens=0, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1), waiting_hint=1)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 45


def test_preflight_passes_waiter_count_to_controller(monkeypatch):
    from app import batch_submission
    seen = {}
    original = batch_submission.check_submission
    def spy(db, **kwargs):
        seen.update(kwargs)
        return original(db, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(batch_submission, 'check_submission', spy)
    import inspect
    assert 'waiting_hint' in inspect.signature(original).parameters
    from app.runtime_admission import preflight_model_pool
    src = inspect.getsource(preflight_model_pool)
    assert 'waiting_hint=int(waiting)' in src and "RuntimeDispatchItem.last_error_code == WAIT_CODE" in src

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_control_windows.py =====
"""Regressions for global collapse, online starvation and wasted image prep."""
import json
from datetime import timedelta
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import pytest
from app.models import ModelQuotaPool, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case, _drive_without_cooldown


def failure(db, number, seconds, code='ServerOverloaded'):
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='failed',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-other', quota_pool_id=1,
        error_code='model_provider_429', upstream_status_code=429,
        reserved_input_tokens=30000, reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1,
        # These are long-tail responses from older requests, not newer admissions.
        created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300), finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds),
        evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure': {'provider_error_code': code}})))
    db.flush()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code,expected_rpm,expected_tpm', [
    ('RequestBurstTooFast', 12, 360000),
    ('ServerOverloaded', 2, 60000),
], ids=['burst-backoff-is-not-idle', 'overload-has-no-backoff-to-wait-out'])
def test_resource_backoff_is_not_a_cold_idle_restart(database, code, expected_rpm, expected_tpm):
    """Replay the observed 240 s admission gap with late 429s throughout it.

    Burst rejections still carry the 5/10/20/40/60 s chain, so the gap is spent
    waiting them out: it must not read as idleness and drop the rate to the
    2 RPM start; the window ends one cut down (pressure), not restarted.

    Since 2026-09-17 ServerOverloaded carries no shared backoff at all, so a
    240 s gap with only overloads landing in it is genuine idleness and the
    ordinary cold restart applies - nothing was holding the pool shut.
    """
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # Insert older physical requests first; only their finishes are late.
        for index, second in enumerate((30, 60, 95, 115, 151, 175)):
            failure(db, 100 + index, second, code=code)
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(
            requests_per_minute=15, tokens_per_minute=450000)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
        db.commit()
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW + timedelta(seconds=240))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_rpm)
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(expected_tpm)
        # Resource wait buys no burst credits or skipped ramp stages.
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW + timedelta(seconds=240))
        later = admit(db, pool, 4, NOW + timedelta(hours=6))
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] <= 2


def test_many_429_cooldowns_in_one_window_cannot_repeatedly_halve_pool(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        p = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        p.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        p.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        db.commit()
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60))
        p.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)
        db.commit()
        next_state = admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=120))
        assert next_state['requests_per_minute'] == state['requests_per_minute']
        assert next_state['tokens_per_minute'] == state['tokens_per_minute']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('hint', [None, 30], ids=['no-hint-no-wait', 'vendor-hint-only'])
def test_late_overload_waits_only_its_vendor_hint_without_resetting_or_halving_healthy_pacing(database, hint):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        failure(db, 100, 40)
        if hint is not None:
            row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            row.evidence_json = json.dumps({'failure': {'provider_error_code': 'ServerOverloaded', 'retry_after_seconds': hint}})
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        db.commit()
        if hint is None:
            # Since 2026-09-17 an overload carries no shared backoff of its own.
            assert check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=41))
        else:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
                check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                    now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=41))
            assert exc.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
            assert exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 29
        next_state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=71))
        assert next_state['requests_per_minute'] == state['requests_per_minute']
        assert next_state['tokens_per_minute'] == state['tokens_per_minute']

def test_long_retry_after_is_not_forgotten_when_feedback_window_rotates(database):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        failure(db, 100, 40)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps({'failure': {'provider_error_code': 'ServerOverloaded', 'retry_after_seconds': 7200}})
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
            check_submission(db, pool=db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool), reserved_tokens=30000,
                now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=600))
        assert exc.value.retry_after_seconds == 6640


def test_warm_pool_has_room_for_daily_inspiration_target_without_ignoring_configured_tpm(database):
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    engine, pool_id = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool_id, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        state = evidence['reservation']['batch_submission']
        state.update(requests_per_minute=120, tokens_per_minute=5000000,
                     pacing_tokens=38178, ramp_started_at=NOW.timestamp())
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
        db.commit()
        # 106.89 calls/minute are needed before retries for 100k/day with
        # 46.08% B short circuit. 0.55 s/request fits that target.
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
        assert check_submission(db, pool=pool, reserved_tokens=204096,
            now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=.55))
        pool.tokens_per_minute = 1000000
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            check_submission(db, pool=pool, reserved_tokens=204096,
                now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=.55))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code,global_cooldown', [('ServerOverloaded', False), ('RequestBurstTooFast', False), ('Unknown429', True)])
def test_real_failure_settlement_keeps_known_batch_cooldown_out_of_online_pool(fallback_case, code, global_cooldown):
    from app.models import ModelConfig
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, fail_model_invocation
    from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route
    from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='scope', name='scope', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='scope', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        row = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], node=kwargs['node'],
            run_snapshot=kwargs['run_snapshot'], endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')
        db.commit()
        liability = row.estimated_cost_micros
        fail_model_invocation(db, invocation_id=row.id, error_code='model_provider_429',
            error_message=code, upstream_status_code=429,
            evidence={'provider_error_code': code, 'retry_after_seconds': 5})
        db.commit()
        assert row.estimated_cost_micros == liability
        assert (pool.cooldown_until is not None) == global_cooldown
        if not global_cooldown:
            assert acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool.id, owner_key='online-fallback').lease_token


@pytest.mark.parametrize('through_release_window', [True, False], ids=['release-window-recorded', 'unrecorded-gap'])
def test_release_window_drain_and_deploy_is_maintenance_not_a_cold_restart(database, through_release_window):
    """2026-09-16 21:54Z / 23:42Z: rpm 63.9 -> 2.0 right after each deploy, ~35 min back to 20.

    hold 首次调用登记 pause、resume 登记 resume 后，二十来分钟的窗口从空闲里扣掉，
    速率保持并按窗口外的时间正常爬坡；没登记的同样间隔仍是冷启动（对照）。
    """
    from app.release_window import release_hold, release_resume
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.desc()))
        evidence = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        evidence['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(
            requests_per_minute=60, tokens_per_minute=1800000, ramp_started_at=NOW.timestamp())
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(evidence)
        db.commit()
        if through_release_window:
            release_hold(db, release='deadbeef', actor='release-window', reason='发布', now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=30))
            db.commit()
            release_resume(db, release='deadbeef', actor='release-window', now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1300))
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1320))
        if through_release_window:
            # 1320 s wall idle minus the 1270 s window leaves 50 s: warm, and the
            # window buys no ramp progress either, so the rate is simply kept.
            assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(60)
            assert state['maintenance_idle_seconds'] == pytest.approx(1270)
        else:
            assert state['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(2)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_endpoint_recovery.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('batch_count,online_count,mode,number', [
    (0, 0, 'batch', 1), (1, 0, 'batch', 2), (2, 0, 'batch', 3),
    (3, 0, 'online', 1), (3, 1, 'online', 2), (3, 2, 'exhausted', None),
])
def test_three_batch_calls_then_bounded_online(batch_count, online_count, mode, number):
    decision = recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW, now=NOW, batch_count=batch_count,
                               online_count=online_count, online_limit=2)
    assert (decision.mode, decision.attempt_no) == (mode, number)


def test_deadline_precedes_unused_batch_retries_and_survives_node_transition():
    for batch_count in range(3):
        decision = recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=24),
            batch_count=batch_count, online_count=0, online_limit=2)
        assert decision.mode == 'online'
    assert recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=24)-timedelta(seconds=7),
        batch_count=1, online_count=0, online_limit=2).remaining_seconds == 7


def test_online_never_switches_back_to_batch_after_clock_adjustment():
    assert recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW, now=NOW, batch_count=1,
        online_count=1, online_limit=2).mode == 'online'


def test_retry_identity_groups_foreach_retry_of_same_child():
    assert recovery.invocation_prefix('item:abc:attempt:1') == recovery.invocation_prefix('item:abc:attempt:2')
    assert recovery.invocation_prefix('item:abc:attempt:1') != recovery.invocation_prefix('item:xyz:attempt:1')


def test_online_route_must_be_frozen_and_cannot_use_batch_endpoint():
    runtime = {'inference_route': {'mode':'batch','endpoint_id':'ep-bi-example',
        'fallback_policy': {'version':'batch-online-fallback-v1','batch_max_attempts':3,
          'deadline_seconds':86400,'online_route': {'mode':'online','endpoint_id':'ep-online'}}}}
    assert recovery.effective_route(runtime, 'online')['endpoint_id'] == 'ep-online'
    assert runtime['inference_route']['endpoint_id'] == 'ep-bi-example'
    runtime['inference_route']['fallback_policy']['online_route']['endpoint_id'] = 'ep-bi-bad'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        recovery.effective_route(runtime, 'online')


def test_executor_uses_three_batch_attempts_then_online_without_restarting_node(monkeypatch):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    calls = []
    def context(**kwargs):
        return recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW, now=NOW,
            batch_count=sum(mode == 'batch' for mode in calls),
            online_count=sum(mode == 'online' for mode in calls), online_limit=2), NOW+timedelta(days=1)
    def attempt(**kwargs):
        calls.append(kwargs['transport_mode'])
        if kwargs['transport_mode'] == 'batch':
            raise executor.FixtureExecutionError('model_provider_transient_parse','invalid JSON',retryable=True)
        if False:
            yield
        return executor.FixtureResult(output_manifest={'ok':True},output_hash='a'*64)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_transport_context', context)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_prompt_attempt_steps', attempt)
    snapshot = {'runtime_context': {'inference_route': {'mode':'batch','fallback_policy': {
        'version':recovery.VERSION,'batch_max_attempts':3,'deadline_seconds':86400}}}}
    operation = executor._real_prompt_steps(run_snapshot=snapshot, node={},
        invocation_key='root', attempt_id=1, lease_token='lease', worker_id='worker')
    while True:
        try:
            next(operation)  # Do not execute the yielded cooldown network-free wait.
        except StopIteration as done:
            assert done.value.output_manifest == {'ok':True}
            break
    assert calls == ['batch','batch','batch','online']


def test_permission_failure_does_not_trigger_paid_retry(monkeypatch):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_transport_context', lambda **_: (
        recovery.decide(queued_at=NOW,now=NOW,batch_count=0,online_count=0,online_limit=1),NOW+timedelta(days=1)))
    def attempt(**kwargs):
        calls.append(1)
        raise executor.FixtureExecutionError('model_provider_non_retryable','forbidden',retryable=False,error_policy_kind='skip')
        yield
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_prompt_attempt_steps', attempt)
    snapshot = {'runtime_context': {'inference_route': {'mode':'batch','fallback_policy': {
        'version':recovery.VERSION,'batch_max_attempts':3,'deadline_seconds':86400}}}}
    with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError, match='forbidden') as error:
        next(executor._real_prompt_steps(run_snapshot=snapshot,node={},attempt_id=1,lease_token='lease',worker_id='worker'))
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert error.value.error_policy_kind == 'fail'


def test_prepare_online_fallback_preserves_parameters_and_batch_run_binding(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import json
    import hashlib
    from copy import deepcopy
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.database import Base
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "prepare-fallback.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        run, attempt, node, model, _ = _case(db,tmp_path,monkeypatch)
        identity = model.model_id
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        runtime = snapshot['runtime_context']
        online = {'mode':'online','endpoint_id':'ep-online-test','foundation_model':identity}
        runtime['inference_route'] = {'mode':'batch','endpoint_id':'ep-bi-test','foundation_model':identity,
            'fallback_policy':{'version':recovery.VERSION,'batch_max_attempts':3,'deadline_seconds':86400,'online_route':online}}
        node = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'][0]
        limit = runtime['real_execution_node_limits'][node['node_instance_id']]
        limit['online_fallback_limits'] = {**deepcopy(limit),'max_attempts':2}
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
        run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
        run.doubao_endpoint_id = 'ep-bi-test'
        db.flush()
        prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(db,run=run,attempt=attempt,node=node,
            input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            invocation_key=recovery.invocation_prefix('root')+'online:1', transport_mode='online')
        assert prepared.client_config.endpoint_id == 'ep-online-test'
        assert prepared.client_config.api_path == '/chat/completions'
        assert run.doubao_endpoint_id == 'ep-bi-test'
        assert prepared.client_config.model_id == identity
        assert prepared.client_config.thinking_mode == 'enabled'
        assert prepared.system_prompt == '冻结系统提示词'
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('experiment', [False, True])
def test_wait_cap_timeout_does_not_exhaust_experiment_evaluation_retries(experiment):
    import json
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    rows = [SimpleNamespace(status='failed', error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
        upstream_status_code=None, invocation_key=f'node:batch:{i}', evidence_json=json.dumps({
            'failure': {'retryable': True, 'technical_error_type': 'timeout',
                        'wait_cap_hit': True, 'wait_cap_seconds': 420.0}})) for i in range(1, 4)]
    decision = recovery.transport_decision(rows, prefix='node:', queued_at=now, now=now,
        online_limit=2, batch_only=experiment)
    assert decision.mode == ('batch' if experiment else 'online')
    if experiment:
        assert decision.attempt_no == 1 and decision.reservation_no == 4


@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', [
    {'retryable': True, 'technical_error_type': 'timeout'},                                   # plain timeout still counts
    {'retryable': False, 'technical_error_type': 'timeout', 'wait_cap_hit': True, 'wait_cap_seconds': 420},
    {'retryable': True, 'technical_error_type': 'network', 'wait_cap_hit': True, 'wait_cap_seconds': 420},
    {'retryable': True, 'technical_error_type': 'timeout', 'wait_cap_hit': True, 'wait_cap_seconds': 120},
])
def test_only_genuine_wait_cap_timeouts_are_exempt(failure):
    import json
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    row = SimpleNamespace(status='failed', error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
        upstream_status_code=None, invocation_key='node:batch:1', evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure': failure}))
    assert recovery.is_wait_cap_timeout(row) is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_experiment.py =====
"""A scoped concurrency experiment cannot spend online money or edit history."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
from app.models import AuditEvent, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError
from test_batch_failure_breaker import shared_case
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route


def start(sessions, case):
    from app.batch_experiment import set_batch_experiment
    with sessions() as db:
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, case['attempt_id'])
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
        frozen = (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash, run.created_at)
        run.status, attempt.status = 'paused', 'retryable'
        event = set_batch_experiment(db, run_id=run.id,
            baseline_run_id=case['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'],
            enabled=True, incident='concurrency-test', actor='test')
        assert set_batch_experiment(db, run_id=run.id,
            baseline_run_id=case['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'],
            enabled=True, incident='concurrency-test', actor='test').id == event.id
        assert (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash, run.created_at) == frozen
        assert db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == 'batch_window_restart'))
        run.status, attempt.status = 'running', 'running'
        db.commit()
        return run.id


def test_only_experiment_materials_keep_batch_after_24_hours(shared_case):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import transport_decision
    from app.batch_experiment import batch_only_experiment
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    identity = start(sessions, cases[0])
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        decisions = []
        for case in cases[:2]:
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, case['attempt_id']).run_id)
            decisions.append(transport_decision([], prefix='node:', now=now,
                queued_at=now-timedelta(days=3), online_limit=2,
                batch_only=batch_only_experiment(db, run)))
        assert [value.mode for value in decisions] == ['batch', 'online']
        assert identity == db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, cases[0]['attempt_id']).run_id


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['online', None])
def test_final_reservation_refuses_online_even_with_stale_transport_decision(shared_case, mode):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    start(sessions, cases[0])
    with sessions() as db:
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as error:
            reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=cases[0]['attempt_id'],
                node=cases[0]['node'], run_snapshot=cases[0]['run_snapshot'],
                endpoint_id=route()['fallback_policy']['online_route']['endpoint_id'] if mode else None,
                transport_mode=mode)
        assert error.value.code == 'model_batch_experiment_hold'
        assert db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all() == []


def test_experiment_does_not_grant_more_batch_attempts_or_replay_online():
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import transport_decision
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    for keys in (['batch:1', 'batch:2', 'batch:3'], ['online:1']):
        rows = [SimpleNamespace(status='failed', error_code='model_provider_json_truncated',
            upstream_status_code=200, invocation_key='node:'+key) for key in keys]
        result = transport_decision(rows, prefix='node:', queued_at=now, now=now,
            online_limit=2, batch_only=True)
        assert result.mode == 'held'

def test_real_transport_context_keeps_experiment_batch_days_later(shared_case, monkeypatch):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    start(sessions, cases[0])
    future = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=3)
    class FutureClock(datetime):
        @classmethod
        def now(cls, tz=None):
            return future
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'datetime', FutureClock)
    decision, deadline = executor._transport_context(**cases[0], invocation_key='root')
    assert decision.mode == 'batch'
    assert deadline == future + timedelta(hours=24)
    other, _ = executor._transport_context(**cases[1], invocation_key='root')
    assert other.mode == 'online'


def test_repeated_maintenance_retries_get_new_physical_ledger_identities(shared_case):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.workflow_model_budget import fail_model_invocation
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    target = cases[0]
    start(sessions,target)
    prefix=invocation_prefix('root')
    historical={}
    for expected_reservation in range(1,5):
        decision,_=executor._transport_context(**target,invocation_key='root')
        assert decision.mode=='batch' and decision.attempt_no==1
        assert decision.reservation_no==expected_reservation
        with sessions() as db:
            key=prefix+'batch:1'+(f':reservation:{expected_reservation}' if expected_reservation>1 else '')
            row=reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=target['attempt_id'],node=target['node'],
                run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'],invocation_key=key,endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'],transport_mode='batch')
            fail_model_invocation(db,invocation_id=row.id,error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
                error_message='fixture maintenance',provider_dispatched=True,
                evidence={'retryable':True,'technical_error_type':'operator_interrupted','operator_incident':'baseline:328:fixture'})
            db.commit()
            historical[row.id]=(row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros,row.evidence_json)
    with sessions() as db:
        rows=db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)).all()
        assert len(rows)==4 and len({r.invocation_key for r in rows})==4
        assert {r.id:(r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.evidence_json) for r in rows}==historical
        assert all(r.status=='failed' and r.actual_cost_micros==0 and r.estimated_cost_micros>0 for r in rows)


def test_experiment_start_requires_drained_correct_scope(shared_case):
    from app.batch_experiment import set_batch_experiment
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    with sessions() as db:
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, cases[0]['attempt_id'])
        kwargs = dict(run_id=step.run_id, baseline_run_id=cases[0]['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'],
            enabled=True, incident='must-not-start', actor='test')
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='paused'):
            set_batch_experiment(db, **kwargs)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='mismatch'):
            set_batch_experiment(db, **{**kwargs, 'baseline_run_id': 987654})
        assert db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == 'batch_experiment_policy')).all() == []


def test_crashed_experiment_hold_preserves_runtime_attempt(shared_case):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem
    from app.workflow_runtime import recover_expired_runtime_steps
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    run_id = start(sessions, cases[0])
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, cases[0]['attempt_id'])
        step.attempt_no = 7
        step.lease_expires_at = now - timedelta(seconds=1)
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == step.id))
        dispatch.status = 'leased'
        dispatch.lease_token = step.lease_token
        dispatch.lease_expires_at = step.lease_expires_at
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run_id, step_attempt_id=step.id,
            invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'online:1', node_instance_id=step.step_key,
            provider='doubao', model_id='test', status='failed', error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
            reserved_input_tokens=100, reserved_output_tokens=20, estimated_cost_micros=999,
            created_at=now-timedelta(minutes=5)))
        db.flush()
        assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=now) == 1
        assert step.status == 'retryable'
        assert step.last_error_code == 'model_batch_experiment_hold'
        assert step.attempt_no == 7
        assert len(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)).all()) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.estimated_cost_micros)) == 999


def test_experiment_restart_keeps_429_backoff_after_old_clock_expires(shared_case):
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.workflow_runtime import _batch_transport_restart
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    run_id = start(sessions, cases[0])
    future = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=3)
    with sessions() as db:
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, cases[0]['attempt_id'])
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run_id, step_attempt_id=step.id,
            invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1', node_instance_id=step.step_key,
            provider='doubao', model_id='test', status='failed', error_code='model_provider_429',
            upstream_status_code=429, reserved_input_tokens=100, reserved_output_tokens=20,
            estimated_cost_micros=999, finished_at=future, created_at=future-timedelta(minutes=5)))
        db.flush()
        decision = _batch_transport_restart(db, db.get(ProductionRun, run_id), step, future)
        assert decision['resource_wait_seconds'] >= 60


def test_policy_activation_serializes_with_another_session_resume(shared_case, monkeypatch):
    from sqlalchemy import text
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
    from app import effective_batch_clock
    from app.batch_experiment import set_batch_experiment, batch_only_experiment
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    with sessions() as db:
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, cases[0]['attempt_id'])
        run_id = step.run_id
        step.status = 'retryable'
        db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status = 'paused'
        db.commit()
    original = effective_batch_clock.append_audit_event
    attempts = []
    def competing_resume(db, **kwargs):
        # Exact race window: eligibility checks passed, first audit INSERT
        # has not occurred. A second database connection must not admit work.
        with sessions() as other:
            other.execute(text('PRAGMA busy_timeout=1'))
            with pytest.raises(OperationalError, match='locked'):
                other.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET status='running' WHERE id=:id"), {'id':run_id})
                other.commit()
            other.rollback()
            attempts.append(True)
        return original(db, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(effective_batch_clock, 'append_audit_event', competing_resume)
    with sessions() as db:
        set_batch_experiment(db, run_id=run_id,
            baseline_run_id=cases[0]['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'],
            enabled=True, incident='race', actor='test')
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert attempts == [True]
        assert batch_only_experiment(db, db.get(ProductionRun, run_id))
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == 'paused'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_experiment_budget.py =====
"""An operator can release a frozen cost gate without rewriting cost or identity."""
import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import AuditEvent, BaselineRegressionRun, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.workflow_model_budget import WorkflowModelBudgetError, reserve_model_invocation
from test_batch_experiment import start
from test_batch_failure_breaker import shared_case, _four_sibling_failures
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route


def capped_case(shared_case):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    # A recoverable provider failure keeps its reserved liability; a confirmed
    # 429 no longer projects into the frozen budget, so it cannot build a cap.
    charges = _four_sibling_failures(sessions, cases, 'model_provider_json_truncated')
    target = cases[4]
    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, target['attempt_id']).run_id)
        target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['real_execution_budget']['max_cost_micros'] = sum(v[1] for v in charges.values())
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(target['run_snapshot'])
        run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
        db.commit()
    start(sessions, target)
    return sessions, target, charges


def reserve(db, target):
    return reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=target['attempt_id'], node=target['node'],
        run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'], endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')


def authorize(db, target, *, enabled=True, incident='operator-release'):
    from app.batch_experiment import set_experiment_cost_gate, baseline_identity
    baseline_id = target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id']
    aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, baseline_id)
    return set_experiment_cost_gate(db, baseline_run_id=baseline_id, disabled=enabled,
        expected_identity=baseline_identity(aggregate), incident=incident, actor='test-operator')


def test_cost_release_admits_batch_and_preserves_frozen_facts_and_unknown_liability(shared_case):
    sessions, target, charges = capped_case(shared_case)
    with sessions() as db, pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
        reserve(db, target)
    assert exc.value.code == 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'
    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, target['attempt_id']).run_id)
        frozen = (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash, run.created_at)
        event = authorize(db, target)
        assert authorize(db, target).id == event.id
        row = reserve(db, target)
        assert row.status == 'reserved' and row.estimated_cost_micros > 0
        assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['cost_gate_override_audit_id'] == event.id
        assert (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash, run.created_at) == frozen
        assert {r.id:(r.status,r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.evidence_json)
            for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.in_(charges)))} == charges


def test_restoring_gate_takes_effect_before_next_reservation(shared_case):
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    with sessions() as db:
        authorize(db, target)
        authorize(db, target, enabled=False, incident='restore')
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
            reserve(db, target)
        assert exc.value.code == 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'


def test_stale_operator_replay_does_not_reenable_a_restored_gate(shared_case):
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    with sessions() as db:
        event = authorize(db, target)
        authorize(db, target, enabled=False, incident='restore')
        assert authorize(db, target).id == event.id
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
            reserve(db, target)
        assert exc.value.code == 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'


def test_no_exemption_for_materials_outside_authorized_batch_experiment(shared_case):
    from app.batch_experiment import experiment_cost_gate_event
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    with sessions() as db:
        authorize(db, target)
        other = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, shared_case[1][0]['attempt_id']).run_id)
        assert experiment_cost_gate_event(db, other, target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id']) is None
        assert experiment_cost_gate_event(db, other, 999999) is None


def test_cost_override_does_not_change_request_limits(shared_case):
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    # Prepare a different immutable fixture, before the operator grants it.
    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, target['attempt_id']).run_id)
        target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['real_execution_budget']['max_requests'] = 4
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(target['run_snapshot'])
        run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
        # Existing experiment identity is deliberately stale; a fresh fixture policy is needed.
        db.query(AuditEvent).delete()
        db.commit()
    start(sessions, target)
    with sessions() as db:
        authorize(db, target)
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
            reserve(db, target)
        assert exc.value.code == 'model_budget_requests_exhausted'


def test_wrong_aggregate_identity_is_rejected_without_writing_authority(shared_case):
    from app.batch_experiment import set_experiment_cost_gate, COST_GATE_ACTION
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    with sessions() as db:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='identity'):
            set_experiment_cost_gate(db, baseline_run_id=target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'],
                disabled=True, expected_identity='0'*64, incident='wrong-target', actor='test')
        assert db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == COST_GATE_ACTION)) is None


def test_cost_policy_and_reservation_are_serialized_on_sqlite(shared_case, monkeypatch):
    from sqlalchemy import text
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
    from app import batch_experiment
    sessions, target, _ = capped_case(shared_case)
    original = batch_experiment.append_audit_event
    blocked = []
    def competing_reservation(db, **kwargs):
        with sessions() as other:
            other.execute(text('PRAGMA busy_timeout=1'))
            with pytest.raises(OperationalError, match='locked'):
                reserve(other, target)
            other.rollback()
            blocked.append(True)
        return original(db, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(batch_experiment, 'append_audit_event', competing_reservation)
    with sessions() as db:
        authorize(db, target)
        db.commit()
    assert blocked == [True]
    with sessions() as db:
        assert reserve(db, target).status == 'reserved'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_experiment_budget_mysql.py =====
"""Cost authority and sibling reservations share a real MySQL locking boundary."""
import json
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Event

from sqlalchemy import select

from app import workflow_model_budget as budget
from app.batch_experiment import set_batch_experiment
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt
from app.native_baseline_incident import resume_baseline_incident
from test_batch_experiment_budget import authorize, reserve
from test_native_baseline_incident_mysql import mysql_incident


def target_for(mysql_incident):
    sessions, contract, _ = mysql_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        restored = resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=1)
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, restored['resumed_run_ids'][0])
        run.status = 'paused'
        db.flush()
        set_batch_experiment(db, run_id=run.id, baseline_run_id=contract['baseline_run_id'],
            enabled=True, incident='mysql-budget-gate', actor='test')
        frozen = (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash)
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        node = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'][0]
        attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == node['node_instance_id']
        ).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc()))
        run.status, attempt.status = 'running', 'running'
        db.commit()
        return sessions, {'attempt_id':attempt.id, 'node':node, 'run_snapshot':snapshot}, frozen


def test_mysql_cost_grant_preserves_frozen_facts_and_immutable_audit(mysql_incident, monkeypatch):
    sessions, target, frozen = target_for(mysql_incident)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, 'REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED', True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, 'MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS', 1)
    with sessions() as db:
        event = authorize(db, target)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        row = reserve(db, target)
        assert row.estimated_cost_micros > 1
        assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['cost_gate_override_audit_id'] == event.id
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, row.production_run_id)
        assert (run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash) == frozen
        db.commit()


def test_mysql_restoring_cost_gate_serializes_a_competing_reservation(mysql_incident, monkeypatch):
    sessions, target, _ = target_for(mysql_incident)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, 'MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS', 1)
    with sessions() as db:
        authorize(db, target)
        db.commit()
    restored, release, requesting = Event(), Event(), Event()
    def operator():
        with sessions() as db:
            authorize(db, target, enabled=False, incident='restore')
            restored.set()
            assert release.wait(10)
            db.commit()
    def worker():
        assert restored.wait(10)
        with sessions() as db:
            requesting.set()
            try:
                reserve(db, target)
                db.commit()
                return 'incorrectly_admitted'
            except budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                return exc.code
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        writer = pool.submit(operator)
        reader = pool.submit(worker)
        try:
            assert requesting.wait(10)
            time.sleep(.15)
            assert not reader.done(), 'reservation bypassed operator aggregate lock'
        finally:
            release.set()
        writer.result(timeout=10)
        assert reader.result(timeout=10) == 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_failure_breaker.py =====
"""run328: sibling provider failures must not preempt frozen bounded recovery."""
import hashlib
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
from app.batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
from app.doubao import DoubaoHTTPError, DoubaoResponse
from app.models import (BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineSetItem,
                        ModelConfig, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowModelInvocation)
from app.workflow_model_budget import (WorkflowModelBudgetError, fail_model_invocation,
                                      real_execution_budget_for_selection, reserve_model_invocation)
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case, _drive_without_cooldown, _drive_requeued
from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route


@pytest.fixture
def shared_case(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('MODEL_FAILURE_STREAK_LIMIT', '4')
    with sessions() as db:
        template = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        original_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        aggregate = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
        aggregate.total = 5
        snapshot = deepcopy(kwargs['run_snapshot'])
        snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['run_modes'] = ['stock_regression']
        runtime = snapshot['runtime_context']
        runtime.update(run_mode='stock_regression', baseline_run_id=aggregate.id, queue_class='validation')
        model = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig))
        runtime['real_execution_budget'] = asdict(real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            snapshot['workflow']['manifest'], 5, model_configs=pricing_models_for_route({model.id:model}, route())))
        run_values = {c.key:getattr(template,c.key) for c in ProductionRun.__table__.columns if c.key != 'id'}
        attempt_values = {c.key:getattr(original_attempt,c.key) for c in ProductionStepAttempt.__table__.columns if c.key != 'id'}
        cases = []
        for index in range(5):
            asset_id = runtime['input_manifest']['asset_id']
            baseline_item = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=aggregate.baseline_set_id, asset_id=asset_id,
                expected_level='L2', expected_result_json='{"level":"L2"}', truth_contract_key='level.v1',
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({'asset_id':asset_id}))
            # Distinct source materials are represented by distinct baseline rows;
            # the local image can be shared because this test targets scheduling.
            if index:
                from app.models import Asset
                asset = db.get(Asset, asset_id)
                copy = Asset(**{c.key:getattr(asset,c.key) for c in Asset.__table__.columns if c.key != 'id'})
                copy.stored_name = f'breaker-{index}.jpg'
                copy.sha256 = hashlib.sha256(str(index).encode()).hexdigest()
                db.add(copy); db.flush()
                baseline_item.asset_id = copy.id
            db.add(baseline_item); db.flush()
            item = BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=aggregate.id, baseline_set_item_id=baseline_item.id,
                asset_id=baseline_item.asset_id, expected_level='L2', status='queued')
            db.add(item); db.flush()
            current = deepcopy(snapshot)
            current['runtime_context']['baseline_item_id'] = item.id
            run = template if index == 0 else ProductionRun(**run_values)
            run.run_key = f'breaker-run-{index}'
            run.idempotency_key = f'breaker-run-{index}'
            run.source_type, run.source_id, run.queue_class = 'baseline_regression_item', item.id, 'validation'
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(current)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            db.add(run); db.flush()
            item.production_run_id = run.id
            attempt = original_attempt if index == 0 else ProductionStepAttempt(**attempt_values)
            attempt.run_id = run.id
            attempt.idempotency_key = f'breaker-attempt-{index}'
            db.add(attempt); db.flush()
            cases.append({**kwargs, 'attempt_id':attempt.id, 'run_snapshot':current})
        db.commit()
    return sessions, cases


def _four_sibling_failures(sessions, cases, code, retryable=True):
    with sessions() as db:
        # Reserve all four before settlement, as with concurrent in-flight calls.
        rows = [reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=case['attempt_id'], node=case['node'],
            run_snapshot=case['run_snapshot'], invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1',
            endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch') for case in cases[:4]]
        for row in rows:
            fail_model_invocation(db, invocation_id=row.id, error_code=code, error_message='fixture provider failure',
                evidence={'retryable':retryable}, upstream_status_code=429 if code == 'model_provider_429' else None)
        db.commit()
        return {row.id:(row.status,row.estimated_cost_micros,row.actual_cost_micros,row.evidence_json) for row in rows}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code', ['model_provider_429', 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown',
    'model_provider_transient_parse', 'model_provider_json_truncated', 'structured_output_schema_mismatch'])
def test_recoverable_sibling_failures_do_not_kill_unissued_material(shared_case, code):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    before = _four_sibling_failures(sessions, cases, code)
    target = cases[4]
    with sessions() as db:
        row = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=target['attempt_id'], node=target['node'],
            run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'], invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1',
            endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')
        assert row.status == 'reserved'
        assert {r.id:(r.status,r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.evidence_json)
            for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.in_(before)))} == before
        assert db.get(ProductionRun,row.production_run_id).status == 'running'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code,retryable', [('model_provider_auth', False), ('model_invocation_outcome_unknown', False)])
def test_nonrecoverable_failures_still_stop_spending(shared_case, code, retryable):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    _four_sibling_failures(sessions, cases, code, retryable)
    target = cases[4]
    with sessions() as db, pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as caught:
        reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=target['attempt_id'], node=target['node'],
            run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'], invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1',
            endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')
    assert caught.value.code == 'model_failure_streak_exhausted'
    assert caught.value.retryable and caught.value.retry_after_seconds == 900


def test_breaker_parks_unissued_material_without_consuming_attempt_or_changing_ledger(shared_case):
    from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem
    from app.workflow_runtime import fail_runtime_step
    sessions,cases=shared_case
    before=_four_sibling_failures(sessions,cases,'model_invocation_outcome_unknown',False)
    target=cases[4]
    with sessions() as db:
        attempt=db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,target['attempt_id'])
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,attempt.run_id)
        snapshot_hash=run.snapshot_hash
        checkpoint=attempt.checkpoint_json
        dispatch=db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id==attempt.id))
        # The shared fixture clones attempts; provide the matching dispatch for
        # this material so the actual runtime failure handler can park it.
        if dispatch is None:
            template=db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem))
            values={c.key:getattr(template,c.key) for c in RuntimeDispatchItem.__table__.columns if c.key!='id'}
            values.update(step_attempt_id=attempt.id)
            dispatch=RuntimeDispatchItem(**values);db.add(dispatch);db.flush()
        original_no=attempt.attempt_no
        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as caught:
            reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=attempt.id,node=target['node'],run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'])
        error=caught.value
        fail_runtime_step(db,attempt.id,attempt.lease_token,error.code,str(error),
            retryable=error.retryable,retry_after_seconds=error.retry_after_seconds)
        assert run.status=='retryable' and attempt.status=='retryable'
        assert attempt.attempt_no==original_no and attempt.checkpoint_json==checkpoint
        assert run.snapshot_hash==snapshot_hash and dispatch.status=='queued'
        assert attempt.lease_token is None and dispatch.lease_token is None
        assert {r.id:(r.status,r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.evidence_json)
            for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))}==before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('expired', [False, True], ids=['three-batch-errors-online', '24h-online'])
def test_sibling_429_cannot_block_frozen_online_fallback(shared_case, expired):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    before = _four_sibling_failures(sessions, cases, 'model_provider_429')
    target = cases[0]  # Its 429 is a resource wait, not an evaluation attempt.
    with sessions() as db:
        for row_id in before:
            db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, row_id).finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(hours=1)
        db.commit()
    if expired:
        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
            aggregate.created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(hours=24,seconds=1)
            db.commit()

    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config

        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
            if self.config.endpoint_id.startswith('ep-bi-'):
                raise DoubaoHTTPError(503, {'Retry-After':'1'})
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                raw_payload={'id':'fake-online'}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='fake-online',
                attempt_count=1, output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)

    result = _drive_requeued(sessions, target, Client)
    assert result.output_manifest['output'] == {'score':88,'label':'通过'}
    with sessions() as db:
        rows = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
            WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,target['attempt_id']).run_id
        ).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).all()
        assert [r.status for r in rows] == (['failed'] if expired else ['failed']*4)+['succeeded']
        assert rows[-1].endpoint_id == 'ep-20260901120000-abcde'
        assert rows[-1].actual_cost_micros == 120
        assert all(db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, row_id).evidence_json == values[3]
                   for row_id, values in before.items())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('code,admitted', [('model_provider_json_truncated', False), ('model_provider_429', True)])
def test_recoverable_failures_keep_liability_but_confirmed_429_is_exempt_from_the_money_cap(shared_case, code, admitted):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    before = _four_sibling_failures(sessions,cases,code)
    target = cases[4]
    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun,db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,target['attempt_id']).run_id)
        target['run_snapshot']['runtime_context']['real_execution_budget']['max_cost_micros'] = sum(v[1] for v in before.values())
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(target['run_snapshot'])
        run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
        db.flush()
        reserve = lambda: reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=target['attempt_id'], node=target['node'],
            run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'], invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1',
            endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch')
        if admitted:
            # The provider refused these at the entrance and billed nothing; the
            # rows keep their reservation facts but do not fill the frozen cap.
            assert reserve().status == 'reserved'
            assert {r.id:(r.status,r.estimated_cost_micros,r.actual_cost_micros,r.evidence_json)
                for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.id.in_(before)))} == before
        else:
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as caught:
                reserve()
            assert caught.value.code == 'model_budget_cost_exhausted'


def test_caller_without_bounded_transport_keeps_breaker_even_with_fallback_snapshot(shared_case):
    sessions,cases = shared_case
    _four_sibling_failures(sessions,cases,'structured_output_schema_mismatch')
    target = cases[4]
    with sessions() as db, pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as caught:
        # Corrections/legacy callers reserve directly, without the transport
        # state machine that owns the batch/online attempt counters.
        reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=target['attempt_id'],node=target['node'],
            run_snapshot=target['run_snapshot'])
    assert caught.value.code == 'model_failure_streak_exhausted'

"""Zero-network recovery tests through preparation, reservation and settlement."""
