===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_job_jsonl.py =====
import base64
import json

import pytest

from app import batch_job_jsonl as wire


def test_materialization_preserves_exact_image_bytes_and_frozen_parameters():
    raw = b"exact previously prepared image bytes"
    body = {"model": "ep-bi-test", "thinking": {"type": "enabled"},
            "response_format": {"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": {"strict": True}},
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "image_url", "image_url": {
                "url": "data:image/png;base64,"+base64.b64encode(raw).decode(), "detail": "high"}}]}]}
    uploaded = []
    def upload(blob, mime, digest):
        uploaded.append((blob, mime))
        return f"tos://private-test/jobs/media/{digest}"
    converted = wire.materialize_images(body, upload=upload, bucket="private-test", prefix="jobs/")
    assert uploaded == [(raw, "image/png")]
    assert converted["thinking"] == body["thinking"]
    assert converted["response_format"] == body["response_format"]
    assert body["messages"][0]["content"][0]["image_url"]["url"].startswith("data:")


def test_partial_jsonl_retains_unfinished_line_and_accepts_out_of_order():
    a = json.dumps({"custom_id": "second", "response": {"status_code": 200, "body": {}}}).encode()+b"\n"
    b = json.dumps({"custom_id": "first", "error": {"code": "failed"}}).encode()+b"\n"
    parser = wire.ResultStream(max_line_bytes=1000)
    assert parser.feed(a+b[:20]) == [json.loads(a)]
    assert parser.feed(b[20:]) == [json.loads(b)]
    assert parser.finish() == []


def test_stream_bounds_memory_and_rejects_duplicate_json_keys():
    oversized = wire.ResultStream(max_line_bytes=10)
    assert oversized.feed(b"x"*11) == []
    assert oversized.error_count == 1
    assert len(oversized.tail) == 0
    parser = wire.ResultStream()
    assert parser.feed(b'{"custom_id":"a","custom_id":"b","error":{}}\n') == []
    assert parser.errors[0]["reason"] == "ValueError"


def test_tos_location_cannot_escape_approved_scope():
    for url in ["https://example.com/image.png", "tos://other/jobs/image.png",
                "tos://private-test/jobs/../elsewhere/image.png",
                "tos://private-test//jobs/image.png"]:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            wire.validate_tos_url(url, bucket="private-test", prefix="jobs/")


def test_request_rows_have_stable_unique_custom_id():
    rows = [("node-a", {"messages": []}), ("node-b", {"messages": []})]
    output = wire.request_jsonl(rows)
    decoded = [json.loads(line) for line in output.splitlines()]
    assert [r["custom_id"] for r in decoded] == [wire.custom_id("node-a"), wire.custom_id("node-b")]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        wire.request_jsonl([rows[0], rows[0]])


def test_bad_line_does_not_swallow_good_results_in_same_chunk():
    good = {"custom_id": "one", "response": {"status_code": 200, "body": {}}}
    data = json.dumps(good).encode()+b'\n{broken}\n'+json.dumps(good).encode()+b'\n'
    parser = wire.ResultStream()
    assert parser.feed(data) == [good, good]
    assert parser.error_count == 1


def test_chunk_boundaries_do_not_change_delivery_even_with_oversized_lines():
    good = {"custom_id": "one", "response": {"body": {}}}
    data = json.dumps(good).encode()+b'\n'+b'x'*300+b'\n{broken}\n'+json.dumps(good).encode()+b'\n'
    for chunk_size in (1, 7, len(data)):
        parser = wire.ResultStream(max_line_bytes=200)
        results = []
        for start in range(0, len(data), chunk_size):
            results.extend(parser.feed(data[start:start+chunk_size]))
        results.extend(parser.finish())
        assert results == [good, good]
        assert parser.error_count == 2

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_job_runtime.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import batch_job_runtime as runtime


NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 10, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def engine(tmp_path):
    value = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'jobs.sqlite'}")
    with value.begin() as connection:
        runtime.initialize(connection)
    yield value
    value.dispose()


def enqueue(db, key, *, since=NOW, group="wv287:A"):
    return runtime.enqueue(db, node_key=key, group_key=group,
                           request_digest="a" * 64, queued_at=since)


def test_partial_results_are_ready_without_whole_batch_barrier(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "fast")
        enqueue(db, "slow")
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=20, now=NOW)
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        assert runtime.record_result(db, node_key="fast", transport="batch",
            call_key=job, result={"grade": "L2"}, usage={"input_tokens": 5}, now=NOW)
        assert [r["node_key"] for r in runtime.ready_results(db)] == ["fast"]
        assert runtime.get_node(db, "slow")["winner"] is None


def test_duplicate_and_late_results_account_but_do_not_replace_winner(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one", since=NOW)
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=20, now=NOW)
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        online = runtime.claim_online(db, limit=1, now=NOW+timedelta(days=1))[0]
        assert runtime.record_result(db, node_key="one", transport="online",
            call_key=online["call_key"], result={"grade": "L1"}, usage={"input_tokens": 7}, now=NOW)
        assert not runtime.record_result(db, node_key="one", transport="batch",
            call_key=job, result={"grade": "L2"}, usage={"input_tokens": 5}, now=NOW)
        assert not runtime.record_result(db, node_key="one", transport="batch",
            call_key=job, result={"grade": "L2"}, usage={"input_tokens": 5}, now=NOW)
        assert len(runtime.receipts(db)) == 2
        assert runtime.ready_results(db)[0]["result"] == {"grade": "L1"}


def test_online_deadline_uses_original_material_enqueue_time(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "A", since=NOW)
        enqueue(db, "B", since=NOW, group="wv287:B")
        assert runtime.claim_online(db, limit=2, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=24)-timedelta(microseconds=1)) == []
        tickets = runtime.claim_online(db, limit=1, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=24))
        assert len(tickets) == 1
        assert runtime.claim_online(db, limit=1, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=25)) == []


def test_unconfirmed_submit_is_durable_and_never_blindly_resubmitted(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=20, now=NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        assert runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=20, now=NOW) is None
        assert runtime.pending_jobs(db)[0]["job_key"] == job
        assert runtime.pending_jobs(db)[0]["status"] == "submitting"


def test_reenqueue_cannot_change_frozen_request_or_reset_clock(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="identity"):
            enqueue(db, "one", since=NOW+timedelta(hours=1))


def test_foreign_result_is_rejected(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="call"):
            runtime.record_result(db, node_key="one", transport="batch", call_key="foreign",
                result={"grade": "L1"}, usage={}, now=NOW)


def test_completed_material_never_falls_back_or_reappears_after_ack(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=20, now=NOW)
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        runtime.record_result(db, node_key="one", transport="batch", call_key=job,
            result={"grade": "L2"}, usage={}, now=NOW)
        runtime.ack_result(db, "one", winner="batch")
        assert runtime.ready_results(db) == []
        assert runtime.claim_online(db, limit=4, now=NOW+timedelta(days=2)) == []


def test_two_connections_cannot_claim_same_online_ticket(engine):
    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        enqueue(db, "two")
    def claim():
        with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
            return runtime.claim_online(db, limit=1, now=NOW+timedelta(days=1))
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        results = list(pool.map(lambda _: claim(), range(2)))
    assert sum(map(len, results)) == 1


def test_provider_terminal_releases_job_slot_but_keeps_missing_results_pending(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=1, now=NOW, max_active_jobs=1)
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        runtime.finish_job(db, job_key=job, remote_id="remote-1", phase="Completed")
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        assert runtime.pending_jobs(db) == []
        assert runtime.get_node(db, "one")["winner"] is None
        enqueue(db, "two")
        assert runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv287:A", limit=1, now=NOW, max_active_jobs=1)


def test_ack_without_valid_winner_cannot_swallow_a_future_result(engine):
    with Session(engine) as db, db.begin():
        enqueue(db, "one")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            runtime.ack_result(db, "one", winner=None)
        assert runtime.get_node(db, "one")["delivered"] == 0


def test_detached_workflow_releases_slot_and_resumes_individual_result(engine, monkeypatch):
    from app.database import Base
    from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeOutboxEvent
    from app import workflow_runtime as workflow
    from app import batch_job_workflow as bridge
    from test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
    from sqlalchemy import select, func
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        run = workflow.create_production_run(db, workflow.ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=version.id, idempotency_key="detached",
            category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="validation",
            runtime_context={}, input_manifest={}, owner="platform"), actor="test")
        attempt_id = workflow.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "worker")
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
        token = attempt.lease_token
        bridge.detach(db, attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=token,
            group_key="wv:A", request_digest="a"*64, material_queued_at=NOW)
        assert attempt.lease_token is None
        assert attempt.status == "retryable"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)) == 0
        assert workflow.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "another") is None
        from app.workflow_runtime_api import _pause_run
        _pause_run(db, run)
        workflow.resume_from_checkpoint(db, run.id)
        assert workflow.claim_next_runtime_step(db, "after-pause") is None
        key = bridge.node_key(attempt_id)
        job = runtime.claim_batch(db, group_key="wv:A", limit=20, now=NOW)
        runtime.confirm_submission(db, job, "remote-1")
        runtime.record_result(db, node_key=key, transport="batch", call_key=job,
            result={"ok": True}, usage={}, now=NOW)
        run_id = run.id
        db.commit()
        def interrupted_ack(*args, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("transaction interrupted before acknowledgement")
        with monkeypatch.context() as patch:
            patch.setattr(runtime, "ack_result", interrupted_ack)
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="interrupted"):
                bridge.resume(db, attempt_id=attempt_id)
            db.rollback()
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id).status == "retryable"
        assert runtime.get_node(db, key)["delivered"] == 0
        assert bridge.resume(db, attempt_id=attempt_id)
        assert run.status == "succeeded"
        assert runtime.ready_results(db) == []
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        from app.models import ProductionRun
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "succeeded"
        assert not bridge.resume(db, attempt_id=attempt_id)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_online_fallback_budget.py =====
import json
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.batch_inference import pricing_models_for_route
from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing
from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection, real_execution_node_limits
from test_native_workflow_real_regression import _priced_model, _prompt

MODEL = 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428'


def route():
    return {'mode': 'batch', 'endpoint_id': 'ep-bi-20260909104719-bkhmk', 'foundation_model': MODEL,
            'fallback_policy': {'version': 'batch-online-fallback-v1', 'batch_max_attempts': 3,
                                'deadline_seconds': 86400,
                                'online_route': {'schema_version': 'inference-route-v1', 'mode': 'online',
                                                 'endpoint_id': 'ep-20260901120000-abcde', 'foundation_model': MODEL}}}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('attempts', [1, 2, 5])
def test_fallback_budget_includes_three_batch_and_original_online_attempts(attempts):
    node = _prompt('a')
    node['config']['model_snapshot'].update(provider='doubao', model_id=MODEL)
    node['retry_policy'] = {'kind': 'fixed', 'max_attempts': attempts}
    manifest = {'nodes': [node]}
    original = deepcopy(manifest)
    model = _priced_model(provider='doubao', model_id=MODEL, pricing_contract_json=json.dumps(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()))
    models = pricing_models_for_route({1: model}, route())
    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(manifest, 1, model_configs=models)
    limits = real_execution_node_limits(manifest, model_configs=models)['a']
    # 1000 input + 200 output costs 660 batch / 1320 online.
    assert budget.expected_cost_micros == 660
    assert budget.max_requests == 3 + attempts
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == 1980 + attempts * 1320
    assert limits['estimated_cost_micros'] == 660
    assert limits['online_fallback_limits']['estimated_cost_micros'] == 1320
    assert limits['online_fallback_limits']['max_attempts'] == attempts
    assert manifest == original


@pytest.mark.parametrize('problem', [None, 'missing', 'disabled', 'unverified', 'wrong_model', 'batch_id', 'base_good', 'base_empty', 'base_wrong'])
def test_new_fallback_routes_require_verified_same_model_online_binding(monkeypatch, problem):
    from sqlalchemy import select
    from app.models import WorkflowDefinition
    from app.doubao_endpoints import create_endpoint, select_inference_route, DoubaoEndpointError
    from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK', 'true')
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        batch = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-bi-20260909104719-bkhmk', display_name='batch', category_key='space_image', foundation_model=MODEL, actor='test')
        online = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-20260901120000-abcde', display_name='online', category_key='space_image', foundation_model=MODEL, actor='test')
        online.verification_status = 'verified'
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = batch.id
        definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = online.id
        if problem == 'missing': definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = None
        if problem == 'disabled': online.status = 'disabled'
        if problem == 'unverified': online.verification_status = 'unverified'
        if problem == 'wrong_model': online.foundation_model = 'other'
        if problem == 'batch_id': definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = batch.id
        if problem in {'base_good', 'base_empty', 'base_wrong'}:
            online.foundation_model = 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite'
            online.model_version = {'base_good': '260428', 'base_empty': '', 'base_wrong': '260215'}[problem]
        if problem and problem != 'base_good':
            with pytest.raises(DoubaoEndpointError):
                select_inference_route(db, definition.id, mode='batch')
        else:
            frozen = select_inference_route(db, definition.id, mode='batch')
            assert frozen['fallback_policy']['online_route']['foundation_model'] == MODEL
            assert frozen['fallback_policy']['online_route']['endpoint_id'] == online.endpoint_id
            online.endpoint_id = 'ep-20260901120000-edcba'
            assert frozen['fallback_policy']['online_route']['endpoint_id'] == 'ep-20260901120000-abcde'
        monkeypatch.delenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK')
        assert 'fallback_policy' not in select_inference_route(db, definition.id, mode='batch')
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

@pytest.mark.parametrize('invalid', [None, 'missing_policy', 'endpoint', 'snapshot', 'batch_quota', 'batch_legacy'])
def test_online_reservation_requires_frozen_route_and_uses_online_price(tmp_path, monkeypatch, invalid):
    import hashlib
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.database import Base
    from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError
    from test_native_baseline_regression import _baseline
    from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism
    from test_native_workflow_real_regression import _attach_priced_model, _available_test_model_secret
    _available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "reserve.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline, workflow = _baseline(db), _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            if invalid in {'batch_quota', 'batch_legacy'}:
                from app.models import ModelQuotaPool
                pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='fallback-test', name='fallback', provider='doubao',
                    scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='test', max_concurrency=1, lease_seconds=90, created_by='test')
                db.add(pool)
                db.flush()
                model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
            model.model_id = MODEL
            model.pricing_contract_json = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
            nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
            snap = next(n for n in nodes if n['kind'] == 'prompt')['config']['model_snapshot']
            snap['model_id'] = MODEL
            snap['runtime_config'] = build_model_config_snapshot(model)
            snap.pop('snapshot_hash')
            snap['snapshot_hash'] = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(snap).encode()).hexdigest()
            workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
            workflow.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)).encode()).hexdigest()
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow,
                actor='test', idempotency_key='reserve', model_execution_mode='real', real_execution_confirmed=True)
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, aggregate.items[0].production_run_id)
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            runtime = snapshot['runtime_context']
            runtime['inference_route'] = route()
            models = pricing_models_for_route({model.id: model}, route())
            manifest = snapshot['workflow']['manifest']
            runtime['real_execution_node_limits'] = real_execution_node_limits(manifest, model_configs=models)
            if invalid in {'missing_policy', 'batch_legacy'}: runtime['inference_route'].pop('fallback_policy')
            production.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            db.commit()
            attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == 'prompt_c'))
            node = next(n for n in manifest['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id'] == 'prompt_c')
            endpoint = route()['fallback_policy']['online_route']['endpoint_id']
            if invalid == 'endpoint': endpoint = 'ep-20260901120000-wrong'
            if invalid == 'snapshot': snapshot['runtime_context']['inference_route']['endpoint_id'] = 'tampered'
            kwargs = dict(attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot,
                          invocation_key='fallback:online:1', endpoint_id=endpoint, transport_mode='online')
            if invalid in {'batch_quota', 'batch_legacy'}:
                from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
                from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
                kwargs.update(transport_mode='batch', endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'])
                started = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
                invocation = reserve_model_invocation(db, **kwargs)
                lease = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease).where(ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == invocation.quota_lease_token))
                expiry = lease.expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                if invalid == 'batch_quota':
                    assert expiry >= started + timedelta(seconds=86700)
                    lease.expires_at = started + timedelta(seconds=60)
                    db.flush()
                    assert reserve_model_invocation(db, **kwargs).id == invocation.id
                    assert lease.expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc) >= started + timedelta(seconds=86700)
                else:
                    assert expiry < started + timedelta(seconds=100)
                assert pool.max_concurrency == 1 and pool.lease_seconds == 90
            elif invalid:
                with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
                    reserve_model_invocation(db, **kwargs)
                assert exc.value.code == ('model_run_snapshot_mismatch' if invalid == 'snapshot' else 'model_online_fallback_invalid')
            else:
                invocation = reserve_model_invocation(db, **kwargs)
                assert invocation.estimated_cost_micros == runtime['real_execution_node_limits']['prompt_c']['online_fallback_limits']['estimated_cost_micros']
                assert invocation.estimated_cost_micros > runtime['real_execution_node_limits']['prompt_c']['estimated_cost_micros']
                assert invocation.endpoint_id == endpoint
                assert reserve_model_invocation(db, **kwargs).id == invocation.id
                assert json.loads(production.snapshot_json) == snapshot
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('remote_version,expected', [('260428', True), ('260215', False), ('', False)])
def test_endpoint_verification_rejects_wrong_or_missing_registered_version(remote_version, expected):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.doubao_endpoints import evaluate_remote_endpoint
    row = SimpleNamespace(foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite', model_version='260428')
    remote = {'Status': 'Running', 'ModelReference': {'FoundationModel': {
        'Name': 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite', 'ModelVersion': remote_version}}}
    ok, detail = evaluate_remote_endpoint(row, remote)
    assert ok is expected
    assert bool(detail['problems']) is (not expected)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_overload_does_not_hold_ramp.py =====
"""ServerOverloaded must not hold the ramp; RequestBurstTooFast still must.

The constant block above `pressure` already says this: measured on 2026-09-12,
ServerOverloaded ran at 16-40% both at 2.4 RPM and at 35-51 RPM, so it tracks the
provider's own load rather than this controller's rate. `overload_rate` was made
observation-only for exactly that reason. `pressure` never got the same
treatment, and counts ServerOverloaded alongside the vendor's burst signal.

That is self-locking at a low rate. A rejection finishes the instant the provider
refuses it, while a batch success finishes minutes later, so the shorter the
window the more it fills with rejections regardless of what the provider is
actually doing. Once the majority rule trips, the rate is cut by a fifth and the
ramp restarts, which shrinks the next window and makes the next trip easier.

Observed on the production gray on 2026-09-15: seven ServerOverloaded against
five successes in one window, rpm walking 2.30 -> 1.84 -> 2.21 and never
climbing, against 2,709 RPM reached by the same pool on 2026-09-11.

Volcengine's own guidance names RequestBurstTooFast as the signal to back off
from, and that path is unchanged: burst sets pressure immediately and keeps the
5/10/20/40/60 second retry_at chain. Since 2026-09-17 ServerOverloaded no
longer feeds that chain either - only its vendor Retry-After is honoured. On
6.9 h of production the chain had kept the whole pool shut 34% of wall time
(89 closures, p90 347 s) because every overload arrives ~5.5 min after its
submission and says nothing about the provider's load now.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.batch_submission import OVERLOAD_MIN_SAMPLE, _feedback_query
from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
from app.models import ModelQuotaPool, WorkflowModelInvocation

from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW


def _completion(db, number, *, status_code, seconds, code=None):
    """One finished batch call inside the feedback window."""
    evidence = {'failure': {'provider_error_code': code}} if code else {}
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt',
        status='failed' if status_code == 429 else 'succeeded',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id='ep-bi-window', quota_pool_id=1,
        error_code='model_provider_429' if status_code == 429 else None,
        upstream_status_code=status_code,
        reserved_input_tokens=30000, reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1,
        created_at=NOW-timedelta(seconds=300), finished_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds),
        evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence)))
    db.flush()


def _window(db, pool_id, *, overloads=0, bursts=0, successes=0):
    number = 100
    for _ in range(overloads):
        _completion(db, number, status_code=429, seconds=number % 100 + 10,
                    code='ServerOverloaded'); number += 1
    for _ in range(bursts):
        _completion(db, number, status_code=429, seconds=number % 100 + 10,
                    code='RequestBurstTooFast'); number += 1
    for _ in range(successes):
        _completion(db, number, status_code=200, seconds=number % 100 + 10); number += 1
    return _feedback_query(db, db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id), NOW+timedelta(seconds=120))


def test_the_observed_production_window_no_longer_holds_the_ramp(database):
    # 2026-09-15 07:02-07:07 exactly: seven overloads against five successes.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        feedback = _window(db, pool, overloads=7, successes=5)
        assert feedback['resource_rejections'] == 7
        assert feedback['completed'] == 12
        assert feedback['pressure'] is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize("overloads,successes", [
    (3, 3), (7, 5), (9, 1), (12, 0), (40, 10),
])
def test_no_amount_of_overload_alone_sets_pressure(database, overloads, successes):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        assert _window(db, pool, overloads=overloads, successes=successes)['pressure'] is False


def test_one_burst_rejection_still_sets_pressure_immediately(database):
    # The vendor's actual burst protection, and the one thing it asked us to
    # slow down for. A single one is enough - no majority, no sample floor.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        assert _window(db, pool, bursts=1, successes=40)['pressure'] is True


def test_a_burst_hidden_among_overloads_still_sets_pressure(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        assert _window(db, pool, overloads=9, bursts=1, successes=6)['pressure'] is True


def test_overload_is_still_measured_for_observation(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        feedback = _window(db, pool, overloads=7, successes=5)
        assert feedback['overload_rate'] == round(7/12, 4)
        assert feedback['resource_rejections'] == 7


def test_a_window_below_the_sample_floor_reports_no_rate(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        feedback = _window(db, pool, overloads=2, successes=1)
        assert feedback['completed'] < OVERLOAD_MIN_SAMPLE
        assert feedback['overload_rate'] == 0.0


def test_overload_spaces_the_next_submission_only_by_its_vendor_hint(database):
    # Three overloads finishing at +10/+11/+12 carry no Retry-After here, so
    # the pool may submit again the instant the last one has finished.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        feedback = _window(db, pool, overloads=3, successes=9)
        assert feedback['pressure'] is False
        assert feedback['retry_at'] == pytest.approx(
            (NOW+timedelta(seconds=12)).timestamp())


def test_burst_rejections_keep_the_escalating_chain(database):
    # The vendor's burst signal still spaces submissions: three bursts
    # finishing at +10/+11/+12 give max(10+5, 11+10, 12+20) = +32.
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        feedback = _window(db, pool, bursts=3, successes=9)
        assert feedback['pressure'] is True
        assert feedback['retry_at'] == pytest.approx(
            (NOW+timedelta(seconds=32)).timestamp())


def test_overload_retry_after_hint_is_still_honoured(database):
    from app.batch_submission import scoped_backoff_seconds
    assert scoped_backoff_seconds('ServerOverloaded', 1, 5) == 1
    assert scoped_backoff_seconds('ServerOverloaded', 7200, 1) == 7200
    assert scoped_backoff_seconds('ServerOverloaded', None, 3) == 0
    assert [scoped_backoff_seconds('RequestBurstTooFast', 1, n) for n in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 9)] == [5, 10, 20, 40, 60, 60]
    assert scoped_backoff_seconds('RequestBurstTooFast', 90, 2) == 90


def _rejected_window(db, pool, code):
    """Drive three admissions across one window that a majority of 429s covers.

    Order matters twice. The rejections are written before any admission so the
    newest batch row stays a real admission carrying pacing evidence - a
    synthetic completion there would be read as the previous state, come back
    empty, and look like a cold start. And the middle admission at +100 s keeps
    each gap inside the 180 s idle threshold, so a single 200 s jump cannot trip
    the cold-idle reset and hide what the ramp actually did.
    """
    for index in range(7):
        _completion(db, 200+index, status_code=429, seconds=25+index, code=code)
    for index in range(5):
        _completion(db, 300+index, status_code=200, seconds=25+index)
    db.commit()
    first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)          # window is still empty here
    admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=100))   # past the 60 s backoff
    return first, admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=200))


def test_the_ramp_climbs_through_an_overloaded_window(database):
    """End to end: the rate must go up, not down, across a 180 s overload window."""
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first, later = _rejected_window(db, pool, 'ServerOverloaded')
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] == pytest.approx(first['requests_per_minute']*1.2)
        assert later['feedback']['pressure'] is False
        assert later['feedback']['overload_rate'] > 0


def test_a_burst_window_still_cuts_the_rate(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first, later = _rejected_window(db, pool, 'RequestBurstTooFast')
        assert later['feedback']['pressure'] is True
        assert later['requests_per_minute'] < first['requests_per_minute']

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_recovery_idle_capacity.py =====
from app.queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy, queue_capacities, DeterministicQueueScheduler


def test_two_batch_retry_long_tails_do_not_block_all_idle_recovery_capacity():
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=48)
    pending = {"recovery": 6}
    running = {"recovery": 2, "validation": 1}
    caps = queue_capacities(policy, pending=pending, running=running)
    assert caps["recovery"].reserved == 2
    # All fresh lanes idle: recovery borrows their idle reserved capacity
    # (48 minus the one running validation item), not just twice its share.
    assert caps["recovery"].effective_limit == 47
    assert DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy).choose_queue(pending=pending, running=running) == "recovery"
    assert DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy).choose_queue(pending=pending, running={"recovery": 4}) == "recovery"
    assert DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy).choose_queue(pending=pending, running={"recovery": 48}) is None


def test_recovery_borrowing_stops_when_fresh_work_is_pending():
    policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=48)
    caps = queue_capacities(policy, pending={"recovery": 6, "production_batch": 1}, running={"recovery": 2})
    assert caps["recovery"].effective_limit == 2
    assert DeterministicQueueScheduler(policy).choose_queue(
        pending={"recovery": 6, "production_batch": 1}, running={"recovery": 4}) == "production_batch"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_fairness.py =====
import inspect
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt, ModelConfig, ModelQuotaPool
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case


def test_shared_turns_reserve_normal_and_new_work_without_idle_capacity():
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import choose_ready
    rows = [dict(ticket=str(i), service=service, continuation=continuation,
                 ready_at=i, attempt_id=i)
            for i, (service, continuation) in enumerate(
                [('high', True)] * 6 + [('high', False)] * 2 + [('normal', True)] * 2, 1)]
    state, selected = None, []
    for _ in range(10):
        winner, state = choose_ready(rows, state)
        selected.append((winner['service'], winner['continuation']))
        rows.remove(winner)
    assert selected[:4] == [('high', True)] * 4
    assert selected[4] == ('normal', True)
    assert selected[5] == ('high', False)
    assert selected.count(('normal', True)) == 2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='state'):
        choose_ready([dict(ticket='x', service='high', continuation=True, ready_at=1, attempt_id=1)], {})


def test_ready_ticket_is_persisted_bound_to_lease_and_not_a_paid_invocation(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready, eligible_ready
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        model = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig))
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
        ticket = register_ready(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
            lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'],
            node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root:batch:2', now=now)
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)) is None
        checkpoint = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).checkpoint_json
        assert kwargs['lease_token'] not in checkpoint
    with sessions() as db:
        rows = eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now)
        assert [row['ticket'] for row in rows] == [ticket]
        assert rows[0]['continuation'] is True
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now+timedelta(seconds=241)) == []
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        attempt.status = 'blocked'
        db.commit()
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now) == []
        attempt.status = 'running'
        attempt.lease_token = 'replacement-lease'
        db.commit()
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=now) == []


def test_no_fairness_registration_when_disabled():
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready
    assert register_ready(None, attempt_id=1, lease_token='', worker_id='',
                          node={}, invocation_key='root') is None


def test_final_pool_gate_requires_prepared_ticket_and_persists_turn(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
    from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='final-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
        ticket = register_ready(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'],
            worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root', now=now)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='no-ticket',
                                batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now)
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease.id)) is None
        db.rollback()
        reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='with-ticket',
            batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now, fair_ticket=ticket)
        assert reservation.submission_state['fairness'] == {'turn':1,'normal':1,'high':0}
        db.rollback()


def test_shared_gate_rejects_losing_ticket_then_borrows_after_winner_cancelled(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import register_ready, check_ready
    from app.model_quota import ModelQuotaUnavailable
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='contended', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='contended', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        first = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        clone = ProductionStepAttempt(**{c.name: getattr(first, c.name)
            for c in ProductionStepAttempt.__table__.columns if c.name != 'id'})
        clone.step_key = 'fair-other'
        clone.idempotency_key = 'fair-other-key'
        db.add(clone)
        db.flush()
        other_id, pool_id = clone.id, pool.id
        db.commit()
        tokens = [register_ready(db, attempt_id=identity, lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'],
            worker_id=kwargs['worker_id'], node=kwargs['node'], invocation_key='root', now=now)
            for identity in (first.id, other_id)]
        db.commit()
    # A separate session sees the same ordering; no process-local arbiter.
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[1], now=now, state=None)
        assert check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[0], now=now, state=None)['normal'] == 1
        db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).status = 'blocked'
        db.commit()
        assert check_ready(db, pool=pool, ticket=tokens[1], now=now, state=None)['normal'] == 1


def test_real_generator_registers_ticket_before_paid_boundary_and_settles(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    import asyncio
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.batch_submission_fairness import eligible_ready
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='consumer-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='consumer', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
    class FakeClient:
        def __init__(self, config):
            self.config = config
        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
            with sessions() as db:
                row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
                assert row.status == 'reserved'
                assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']['fairness']['turn'] == 1
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                raw_payload={'id':'fake'}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='fake',
                attempt_count=1, output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)
    async def scenario():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1, phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1)) as runtime:
            return await runtime.run(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=FakeClient))
    result = asyncio.run(scenario())
    assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.status == 'succeeded'
        assert eligible_ready(db, pool_id=pool_id, now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)) == []


def test_paid_correction_rerun_is_exempt_from_the_shared_ticket_queue(fallback_case, monkeypatch):
    """人工纠偏的付费重跑没有票据，也不该被公平队列挡住。

    2026-09-20：运营在 .137 对冻结在批量路线上的素材做字段纠偏，每次都收到
    「批量准备票据缺失或已过期，等待重新准备」。票据只有 Worker 在准备阶段登记，
    纠偏是在接口里同步重跑的，按定义就没有票据，于是 100% 失败。
    """
    from app.batch_submission import check_submission
    from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
    from app.models import ModelQuotaLease
    sessions, _kwargs = fallback_case
    monkeypatch.setenv('WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION', '1')
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='exempt-fair', name='fair', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='fair', created_by='test', max_concurrency=8)
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
    with sessions() as db:
        # 没票据又不豁免：照旧挡回去，自动链路的排队语义不变
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as blocked:
            acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='worker-no-ticket',
                                batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now)
        assert blocked.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease.id)) is None
        db.rollback()
        # 人工纠偏豁免：拿到名额，且不写入任何轮转状态（它本来就不在队里）
        reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key='paid-correction',
            batch_submission=True, reserved_tokens=1, now=now, fairness_exempt=True)
        assert 'fairness' not in reservation.submission_state
        db.rollback()
    # 豁免只跳过票据轮转，爬坡节流那一段照常
    assert 'fairness_exempt' in inspect.getsource(check_submission)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_mysql.py =====
"""Real process competition in the explicitly disposable MySQL test schema."""
import json
import multiprocessing
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, release_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, WorkflowModelInvocation
NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 11, 4, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _submit(url, pool_id, run_id, attempt_id, now, start, ready_queue, result_queue):
    if url.host != '127.0.0.1' or not url.database.startswith('labelsystem_test_'):
        raise ValueError('only disposable loopback schemas allowed')
    engine = create_engine(url)
    event.listen(engine, 'connect', lambda c, _: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(c))
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.connection()  # Establish every process before releasing the start barrier.
            ready_queue.put(attempt_id)
            assert start.wait(120), 'parent did not finish starting the isolated processes'
            try:
                quota = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id, owner_key=f'call:{attempt_id}',
                    reserved_tokens=204096, now=now, batch_submission=True,
                    submission_estimate={'tokens':15000,'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})
                db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run_id, step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    node_instance_id='prompt', invocation_key='root', provider='doubao', model_id='test',
                    endpoint_id=f'ep-bi-category-{attempt_id%2}', quota_pool_id=pool_id,
                    quota_lease_token=quota.lease_token, reserved_input_tokens=204096,
                    reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1, created_at=now,
                    evidence_json=json.dumps({'reservation': {'batch_submission': quota.submission_state}})))
                release_model_quota(db, lease_token=quota.lease_token, now=now)
                db.commit()
                result_queue.put('admitted')
            except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                db.rollback()
                result_queue.put(exc.code)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_processes_share_batch_ramp_after_restart(mysql_engine):
    # Keep fixture imports in the parent. Spawn workers only need the real
    # quota/model code, not FastAPI and every test helper's transitive imports.
    from test_model_quota import _pool
    from test_runtime_long_tail_scheduling import ready
    from test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
    import time
    with mysql_engine.begin() as conn:
        create_mysql_baseline(conn)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
        pool = _pool(db, key='pacing-mysql', concurrency=24)
        pool_id = pool.id
        targets = []
        for i in range(8):
            attempt, _ = ready(db, version, f'pacing-{i}')
            targets.append((attempt.run_id, attempt.id))
        db.commit()
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context('spawn')
    for wave in range(2):
        start, ready_queue, results = ctx.Event(), ctx.Queue(), ctx.Queue()
        children = [ctx.Process(target=_submit, args=(mysql_engine.url, pool_id,
            *targets[wave*4+i], NOW+timedelta(seconds=30*wave), start, ready_queue, results)) for i in range(4)]
        try:
            for child in children: child.start()
            deadline = time.monotonic()+120
            for _ in children:
                try:
                    ready_queue.get(timeout=max(1, deadline-time.monotonic()))
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise AssertionError(f'isolated worker startup failed: {[c.exitcode for c in children]}') from exc
            start.set()
            outcomes = [results.get(timeout=30) for _ in children]
            assert outcomes.count('admitted') == 1
            assert outcomes.count('model_batch_submission_wait') == 3
            for child in children:
                child.join(timeout=20)
                assert child.exitcode == 0
        finally:
            for child in children:
                if child.is_alive(): child.terminate()
                child.join(timeout=5)
            ready_queue.close()
            results.close()
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(ModelQuotaLease.status == 'active')) == 0
        for row in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)):
            state = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']
            assert row.reserved_input_tokens == state['reserved_tokens'] == 204096
            assert state['pacing_tokens'] == 15000

===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_submission_pacing.py =====
"""Shared submission pacing is distinct from in-flight concurrency (no API calls)."""
import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.model_quota import acquire_model_quota, ModelQuotaUnavailable, release_model_quota
from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaPool, WorkflowModelInvocation
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case, _drive_without_cooldown

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 11, 4, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def database(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "pacing.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='batch-pacing', name='batch', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='test', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.commit()
        pool_id = pool.id
    yield engine, pool_id
    engine.dispose()


def admit(db, pool_id, number, now, *, tokens=30000, endpoint='ep-bi-test', estimate=None):
    reservation = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id,
        owner_key=f'call:{number}', reserved_tokens=tokens, now=now,
        batch_submission=True, submission_estimate=estimate)
    # Same transaction as the production invocation writer: a second process
    # cannot acquire the pool before this submission evidence is committed.
    db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=number, step_attempt_id=number,
        invocation_key='root', node_instance_id='prompt', status='reserved',
        provider='doubao', model_id='test', endpoint_id=endpoint, quota_pool_id=pool_id,
        reserved_input_tokens=tokens, reserved_output_tokens=0, estimated_cost_micros=1,
        created_at=now, evidence_json=json.dumps({'reservation': {
            'batch_submission': reservation.submission_state}})))
    release_model_quota(db, lease_token=reservation.lease_token, now=now)
    db.commit()
    return reservation.submission_state


def test_idle_slots_do_not_allow_simultaneous_submissions_across_sessions(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 2, NOW, endpoint='ep-bi-another-category')
        assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 30
        # Even a caller committing after denial cannot leave a phantom lease.
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
        assert admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30))


def test_heavy_request_uses_shared_slot_and_delays_following_request_online_bypasses(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=90000)
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60), tokens=30000)
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 60
        db.rollback()
        online = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='online', now=NOW)
        assert online.submission_state is None


def test_restart_does_not_reset_warmup_and_idle_does_not_accumulate_credit(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        later = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30))
        assert later['ramp_started_at'] == first['ramp_started_at']
        after_idle = admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(hours=6))
        assert after_idle['requests_per_minute'] <= first['requests_per_minute']
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
            admit(db, pool, 4, NOW+timedelta(hours=6))


def test_live_warmup_grows_no_more_than_twenty_percent_per_three_minutes(database):
    engine, pool = database
    history = []
    now = NOW
    for number in range(1, 31):
        while True:
            with Session(engine) as db:
                try:
                    state = admit(db, pool, number, now)
                    break
                except ModelQuotaUnavailable as exc:
                    assert exc.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
                    now += timedelta(seconds=exc.retry_after_seconds)
        if history:
            previous = history[-1]
            for field in ('requests_per_minute', 'tokens_per_minute'):
                assert state[field] <= previous[field]*1.2+1e-6
            if state['tokens_per_minute'] > previous['tokens_per_minute']:
                assert state['ramp_started_at']-previous['ramp_started_at'] >= 180
        history.append(state)
    assert history[-1]['tokens_per_minute'] > history[0]['tokens_per_minute']


def test_new_provider_cooldown_reduces_rate_and_restart_keeps_reduction(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        row.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        row.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        recovered = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=60))
        assert recovered['tokens_per_minute'] <= state['tokens_per_minute']/2
        assert recovered['requests_per_minute'] <= state['requests_per_minute']/2
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as caught:
            admit(db, pool, 3, NOW+timedelta(seconds=61))
        assert caught.value.retry_after_seconds >= 59


def test_rereading_existing_reservation_does_not_pace_or_double_charge(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        first = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='existing',
            reserved_tokens=30000, now=NOW, batch_submission=True)
        db.commit()
        replay = acquire_model_quota(db, quota_pool_id=pool, owner_key='existing',
            reserved_tokens=30000, now=NOW, batch_submission=True)
        assert replay.reused and replay.lease_token == first.lease_token
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('expired', [False, True])
def test_real_executor_parks_until_deadline_without_dispatch_or_attempt_charge(fallback_case, expired):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ModelConfig, ProductionStepAttempt, ProductionRun, RuntimeDispatchItem
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='pacer', name='pacer', provider='doubao',
            scope_kind='account', scope_identifier='pacer', max_concurrency=24,
            lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, 7001)
        event.received_at = now-timedelta(hours=24)+timedelta(seconds=-1 if expired else 5)
        admit(db, pool.id, 9999, now)
        original_hash = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).run_id).snapshot_hash
    calls = []
    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        async def chat_json(self, *args, **options):
            calls.append(self.config.endpoint_id)
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88, 'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{}', raw_payload={},
                upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='offline', attempt_count=1,
                output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_tokens=80, output_tokens=20, total_tokens=100)
    if expired:
        result = _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
        assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88
        assert len(calls) == 1 and not calls[0].startswith('ep-bi-')
    else:
        with pytest.raises(executor.FixtureExecutionError) as caught:
            _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
        assert caught.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        assert 0 < caught.value.retry_after_seconds <= 5
        assert calls == []
        executor._report_runtime_step_failure_with_retry(attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'],
            lease_token=kwargs['lease_token'], exc=caught.value)
        with sessions() as db:
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'retryable' and attempt.attempt_no == 1 and attempt.lease_token is None
            dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id))
            assert dispatch.status == 'queued' and dispatch.lease_token is None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id).snapshot_hash == original_hash

def test_required_long_spacing_is_not_mistaken_for_idle_and_can_recover(database):
    engine, pool = database

def test_financial_upper_bound_does_not_delay_small_prepared_request(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=204096,
            estimate={'tokens': 15000, 'basis': 'prepared-request-v1'})

def test_cooldown_recovery_clock_is_not_reset_by_denied_checks(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=600000)
        row = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        row.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        row.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'
        db.commit()
    for seconds in (60, 180, 240):
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
                admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds))
            db.rollback()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # After cooldown + 180 seconds recovery has begun even though no
        # reservation could yet persist the observed cooldown.
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000))
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] == 36000
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 1.2


@pytest.mark.parametrize('settled', [False, True])
def test_v1_upgrade_keeps_rate_and_uses_only_known_usage_for_previous_weight(database, settled):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=204096)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        state = ev['reservation']['batch_submission']
        state['version'] = 'batch-submission-pacing-v1'
        state.pop('pacing_tokens', None)
        state.pop('estimate', None)
        state.pop('algorithm_version', None)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(ev)
        if settled:
            row.status, row.input_tokens, row.output_tokens = 'succeeded', 10000, 500
        db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        if settled:
            state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=204096)
            assert state['requests_per_minute'] == 2
        else:
            with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable):
                admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=204096,
                    estimate={'tokens':15000, 'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})


def test_prepared_token_estimate_excludes_base64_and_includes_schema_and_each_image():
    from types import SimpleNamespace as N
    from app.batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
    body = {'messages':[{'role':'user','content':[
        {'type':'text','text':'测试'},
        {'type':'image_url','image_url':{'url':'data:image/jpeg;base64,'+'A'*100000}}]}],
        'response_format':{'schema':{'description':'字段说明'}},'max_tokens':4096}
    prepared = N(serialized_provider_request=N(body=json.dumps(body).encode()),
        output_budget=4096, media_dispatch_identities=('target:0',),
        media_bundle=N(items=(N(role='target',ordinal=0,image_tokens=1280),)))
    estimate = estimate_prepared_submission(prepared)
    assert 5376 < estimate['tokens'] < 6500
    assert estimate['text_utf8_bytes'] > len('测试字段说明'.encode())
    assert estimate['image_tokens'] == 1280
    prepared.media_bundle.items[0].image_tokens = 0
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None
    prepared.serialized_provider_request = None
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None


def test_explicit_multiframe_and_anchor_images_each_contribute_tokens():
    from types import SimpleNamespace as N
    from app.batch_submission import estimate_prepared_submission
    image = {'type':'image_url','image_url':{'url':'data:image/jpeg;base64,aaa'}}
    prepared = N(serialized_provider_request=N(body=json.dumps({'messages':[{'content':[image]*3}]}).encode()),
        output_budget=1000, media_dispatch_identities=('target:0','target:1','anchor:0'),
        media_bundle=N(items=(N(role='target',ordinal=0,image_tokens=1280),
            N(role='target',ordinal=1,image_tokens=1280),N(role='anchor',ordinal=0,image_tokens=595))))
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared)['image_tokens'] == 3155
    prepared.media_dispatch_identities = ('target:0',)
    assert estimate_prepared_submission(prepared) is None


def test_unknown_pacing_algorithm_fails_closed(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        ev['reservation']['batch_submission']['algorithm_version'] = 'corrupt'
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(ev); db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
            admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(hours=1))
        assert exc.value.code == 'batch_submission_evidence_invalid'


def test_zero_token_quota_still_obeys_rpm_and_does_not_poison_next_admission(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW, tokens=0)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        with pytest.raises(ModelQuotaUnavailable) as exc:
            admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=1), tokens=0)
        assert exc.value.code == 'model_batch_submission_wait'
        db.rollback()
        admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(seconds=30), tokens=0)


def test_expired_new_cooldown_cannot_skip_idle_cold_start(database):
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)
        ev['reservation']['batch_submission'].update(requests_per_minute=100,tokens_per_minute=1000000)
        row.evidence_json = json.dumps(ev)
        quota = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool)
        quota.cooldown_until = NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
        quota.cooldown_reason = 'provider_rate_limited'; db.commit()
    with Session(engine) as db:
        state = admit(db, pool, 2, NOW+timedelta(hours=6))
        assert state['requests_per_minute'] <= 2
        assert state['tokens_per_minute'] <= 60000


def test_budget_ledger_remains_frozen_with_independent_submission_estimate(fallback_case):
    from app.models import ModelConfig
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from test_batch_online_fallback_budget import route
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='split', name='split', provider='doubao', scope_kind='account',
            scope_identifier='split', max_concurrency=24, lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool); db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        before = json.dumps(kwargs['run_snapshot'], sort_keys=True)
        row = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=kwargs['attempt_id'], node=kwargs['node'],
            run_snapshot=kwargs['run_snapshot'], endpoint_id=route()['endpoint_id'], transport_mode='batch',
            submission_estimate={'tokens':1000,'basis':'prepared-request-v1'})
        ev = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']
        assert ev['max_input_tokens'] == row.reserved_input_tokens
        assert ev['max_output_tokens'] == row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert ev['estimated_cost_micros'] == row.estimated_cost_micros
        assert ev['batch_submission']['pacing_tokens'] == 1000
        assert row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens > 1000
        assert json.dumps(kwargs['run_snapshot'], sort_keys=True) == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('fallback_case', [True], indirect=True)
def test_real_explicit_image_executor_persists_traffic_estimate_and_original_budget(fallback_case):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.models import ModelConfig, ProductionRun
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    with sessions() as db:
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key='prepared', name='prepared', provider='doubao', scope_kind='account',
            scope_identifier='prepared', max_concurrency=24, lease_seconds=900, created_by='test')
        db.add(pool); db.flush()
        db.scalar(select(ModelConfig)).quota_pool_id = pool.id
        original_hash = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_hash
        db.commit()
    calls = []
    class Client:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        async def chat_json_images(self, *args, **options):
            calls.append(options)
            assert options['prepared_request'].sample_count == 1
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'score':88,'label':'通过'}, raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"通过"}',
                raw_payload={'id':'fake-batch'}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id='fake-batch',
                output_budget=options['output_budget'], reasoning_effort=options['reasoning_effort'],
                thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, attempt_count=1,
                input_tokens=1000, output_tokens=50, total_tokens=1050)
    result = _drive_without_cooldown(executor._real_prompt_steps(**kwargs, model_client_type=Client))
    assert result.output_manifest['output']['score'] == 88 and len(calls) == 1
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
        assert row.status == 'succeeded'
        state = json.loads(row.evidence_json)['reservation']['batch_submission']
        assert 0 < state['pacing_tokens'] < row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert state['estimate']['image_count'] == 1 and state['estimate']['image_tokens'] > 0
        assert state['reserved_tokens'] == row.reserved_input_tokens+row.reserved_output_tokens
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).snapshot_hash == original_hash
