===== FILE backend/tests/test_batch_worker_crash_settlement.py =====
"""A killed batch waiter must settle unknown after the worker liveness lease."""
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem,
                        WorkflowModelInvocation, ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaPool,
                        Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem, BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineRegressionItem)


def _waiting_worker(root):
    # This is a genuine separate process. No provider or external network is possible.
    def deny(*args, **kwargs):
        raise AssertionError('external networking forbidden in crash fixture')
    socket.socket.connect = deny
    socket.socket.connect_ex = deny
    socket.create_connection = deny
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case
    from app.audit import canonical_json
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    engine = create_engine('sqlite:///' + str(root / 'crash.sqlite'))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    executor.SessionLocal = sessions
    with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as patch:
        with sessions() as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(db, root, patch)
            pool = ModelQuotaPool(pool_key="crash-fixture", name="crash-fixture", provider="doubao",
                                  scope_kind="account", scope_identifier="fixture", max_concurrency=2, lease_seconds=86460, created_by="test")
            db.add(pool); db.flush()
            model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
            model.timeout_seconds = 86400
            node['retry_policy']['max_attempts'] = 3
            frozen_model = node['config']['model_snapshot']
            frozen_model['runtime_config']['timeout_seconds'] = 86400
            frozen_model.pop('snapshot_hash', None)
            frozen_model['snapshot_hash'] = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(frozen_model).encode()).hexdigest()
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'][0] = node
            asset = db.scalar(select(Asset))
            baseline = BaselineSet(name='crash-fixture', category_key=run.category_key,
                                   default_expected_level='L2', fingerprint='a'*64, created_by='test')
            db.add(baseline); db.flush()
            sample = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id,
                                     expected_level='L2', asset_snapshot_json='{}')
            db.add(sample); db.flush()
            parent = BaselineRegressionRun(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, sequence_no=1,
                workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id, execution_engine='native_workflow',
                category_key=run.category_key, strategy_snapshot_json='{}',
                baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline.fingerprint, total=1, created_by='test')
            db.add(parent); db.flush()
            item = BaselineRegressionItem(run_id=parent.id, baseline_set_item_id=sample.id,
                asset_id=asset.id, expected_level='L2', production_run_id=run.id)
            db.add(item); db.flush()
            run.source_id = item.id
            snapshot['runtime_context']['baseline_run_id'] = parent.id
            snapshot['runtime_context']['baseline_item_id'] = item.id
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(run.snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest()
            attempt_id, token = attempt.id, attempt.lease_token
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            db.commit()

        class WaitingClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                assert config.timeout_seconds == 86400

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                # The real executor has committed its invocation and heartbeat by here.
                (root / 'waiting.json').write_text(json.dumps({
                    'at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                    'attempt_id': attempt_id,
                    'token': token,
                }))
                await asyncio.Event().wait()

        executor._real_prompt_result(
            worker_id='worker-1', attempt_id=attempt_id, lease_token=token,
            run_snapshot=snapshot, node=node, input_manifest=input_manifest,
            model_client_type=WaitingClient,
        )


def test_killed_batch_waiter_recovers_at_301_seconds_without_resend(tmp_path):
    backend = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    child_env = dict(os.environ)
    child_env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join([str(backend), str(backend / "tests"), child_env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")])
    worker = subprocess.Popen(
        [sys.executable, __file__, '--waiting-worker', str(tmp_path)],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, env=child_env,
    )
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 20
        marker = tmp_path / 'waiting.json'
        while not marker.exists() and worker.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(.05)
        if not marker.exists():
            worker.kill()
            stdout, stderr = worker.communicate(timeout=5)
            pytest.fail(f'worker never entered model wait: {stdout} {stderr}')
        waiting = json.loads(marker.read_text())
        engine = create_engine('sqlite:///' + str(tmp_path / 'crash.sqlite'))
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation.status == 'reserved'
            estimate = invocation.estimated_cost_micros
            assert estimate > 0
            invocation_id = invocation.id
            quota = db.scalar(select(ModelQuotaLease))
            assert quota is not None and quota.status == 'active'
            assert (quota.expires_at - quota.acquired_at).total_seconds() == 86460
        worker.kill()
        worker.wait(timeout=5)
        assert worker.returncode != 0
        recovery_now = datetime.fromisoformat(waiting['at']) + timedelta(seconds=301)
        from app.workflow_runtime import recover_expired_runtime_steps, claim_next_runtime_step
        with Session(engine) as db:
            recovered = recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=recovery_now)
            db.commit()
            assert recovered == 1, 'dead worker still owns a batch-duration runtime lease'
        with Session(engine) as db:
            invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
            assert invocation.status == 'failed'
            assert invocation.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert invocation.estimated_cost_micros == estimate
            # Baseline runs retain the unknown estimate in their invocation ledger;
            # the incremental-only daily-budget table is not used by this source.
            assert invocation.reserved_input_tokens > 0 and invocation.reserved_output_tokens > 0
            assert invocation.actual_cost_micros == 0
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, waiting['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'failed' and attempt.lease_token is None
            assert attempt.lease_expires_at is None
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id)
            assert run.status == 'failed' and run.error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert run.lease_expires_at is None
            parent = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
            item = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionItem))
            assert parent.status == 'failed' and parent.completed == parent.failed == 1
            assert item.status == 'failed' and item.error_message == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
            assert json.loads(item.result_snapshot_json)['normalized_result'] is None
            assert all(d.status not in {'leased','queued'} for d in db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem)))
            quotas = list(db.scalars(select(ModelQuotaLease)))
            assert len(quotas) == 1 and quotas[0].status == 'released'
            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=recovery_now + timedelta(days=2)) == 0
            assert claim_next_runtime_step(db, 'replacement-worker', now=recovery_now, global_limit=2) is None
            db.commit()
            assert len(list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))) == 1
            assert len(list(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt)))) == 1
            assert json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json) == {}
            assert run.status == 'failed'
        from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step, WorkflowRuntimeError
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as stale:
                complete_runtime_step(db, waiting['attempt_id'], waiting['token'], {'score':88,'label':'late'}, now=recovery_now)
            assert stale.value.code in {'step_lease_stale', 'run_terminal_state'}
            db.rollback()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, waiting['attempt_id'])
            assert attempt.status == 'failed' and json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json) == {}
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, attempt.run_id).status == 'failed'
            assert db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id).error_code == 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
        engine.dispose()
    finally:
        if worker.poll() is None:
            worker.kill()
        worker.communicate(timeout=5)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    assert sys.argv[1] == '--waiting-worker'
    _waiting_worker(Path(sys.argv[2]))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_billing_local_aggregate.py =====
"""Keep billing status and Beijing-day boundaries intact across SQL dialects."""
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.volcengine_billing import local_day_aggregate


def test_local_billing_excludes_rejections_and_unsettled_costs():
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with Session(engine) as db:
        # This read model only needs these columns; no model/provider invocation.
        db.execute(text('''CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, endpoint_id TEXT, created_at DATETIME,
            status TEXT, error_code TEXT, input_tokens INTEGER, output_tokens INTEGER,
            cached_input_tokens INTEGER, actual_cost_micros INTEGER, upstream_status_code INTEGER)'''))
        rows = [
            (1, 'ep-test', '2026-09-20 16:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 100, 20, 10, 7, 200),
            # 带实际用量结算的失败行（如 schema 不符）：token 与费用都要计入
            (2, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 15:59:59.999999', 'failed', None, 50, 10, 0, 27, 200),
            # 未结算：reserved 行的费用不计
            (3, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'reserved', 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown', 0, 0, 0, 999, None),
            (4, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'failed', 'model_invocation_outcome_unknown', 0, 0, 0, 0, None),
            (5, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'rejected', None, 0, 0, 0, 999, None),
            (6, 'ep-other', '2026-09-21 01:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            (7, 'ep-test', '2026-09-20 15:59:59.999999', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            (8, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 16:00:00.000000', 'succeeded', None, 1, 1, 0, 500, 200),
            # 供应商 429 拒绝：确认未计费，不算调用次数
            (9, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21 02:00:00.000000', 'failed', 'model_provider_429', 0, 0, 0, 0, 429),
        ]
        db.connection().exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)', rows)
        assert local_day_aggregate(db, 'ep-test', '2026-09-21') == {
            'calls': 4, 'input_tokens': 150, 'output_tokens': 30,
            'cached_input_tokens': 10, 'cost_micros': 7 + 27, 'unresolved_calls': 2,
        }
        assert local_day_aggregate(db, 'ep-empty', '2026-09-21') == {
            'calls': 0, 'input_tokens': 0, 'output_tokens': 0,
            'cached_input_tokens': 0, 'cost_micros': 0, 'unresolved_calls': 0,
        }
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_bounded_runtime.py =====
import asyncio
import threading
import time

import pytest


def test_native_executor_has_async_entry_point():
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor
    assert callable(getattr(workflow_fixture_executor, 'process_runtime_step_async', None))


def test_waiting_requests_release_phase_threads_and_obey_bound():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait

    async def scenario():
        live = peak = 0
        phase_threads = set()
        async def provider():
            nonlocal live, peak
            live += 1
            peak = max(peak, live)
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(.03)
                return 7
            finally:
                live -= 1
        def operation():
            phase_threads.add(threading.get_ident())
            result = yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=1)
            phase_threads.add(threading.get_ident())
            return result
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=4, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            results = await asyncio.gather(*(runtime.run(operation()) for _ in range(12)))
        assert results == [7] * 12
        assert peak == 4  # A phase thread must not be held for each HTTP wait.
        assert len(phase_threads) == 1
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_cancellation_and_timeout_are_delivered_to_settlement():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait

    async def scenario():
        settled = []
        dispatched = asyncio.Event()
        async def provider():
            dispatched.set()
            await asyncio.Event().wait()
        def operation(timeout):
            try:
                yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=timeout)
            except Exception:
                settled.append('unknown-no-replay')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation(30)))
            await dispatched.wait()
            task.cancel()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
            await runtime.run(operation(.01))
        assert settled == ['unknown-no-replay', 'unknown-no-replay']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_cancellation_during_preparation_finishes_phase_before_cleanup():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        started, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            facts.append('dispatched')
        def operation():
            try:
                started.set()
                release.wait(2)
                facts.append('prepared')
                yield ProviderWait(provider, timeout_seconds=1)
            except Exception:
                facts.append('settled')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            task = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(operation()))
            while not started.is_set():
                await asyncio.sleep(.001)
            task.cancel()
            release.set()
            with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await task
        assert facts == ['prepared', 'settled']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_provider_heartbeat_failure_stops_wait_and_settles():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            try:
                await asyncio.Event().wait()
            finally:
                facts.append('http-closed')
        def heartbeat():
            raise RuntimeError('lease lost')
        def operation():
            try:
                yield ProviderWait(provider, 5, heartbeat, .01)
            except RuntimeError:
                facts.append('unknown')
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            await runtime.run(operation())
        assert facts == ['http-closed', 'unknown']
    asyncio.run(scenario())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case', [
    'test_real_native_worker_calls_one_model_and_completes_local_nodes',
    'test_materialized_design_case_real_graph_calls_two_images_then_case_prompt',
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('split', [False, True])
def test_async_native_business_settlement(tmp_path, monkeypatch, case, split):
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    import test_native_workflow_real_regression as contracts
    if not hasattr(contracts, case):
        pytest.fail('real business contract name must exist: ' + case)
    contracts._available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    def execute(worker_id, **kwargs):
        from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
        from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
        async def run():
            async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                    phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1) if split else None) as runtime:
                return await executor.process_runtime_step_async(worker_id, runtime=runtime, **kwargs)
        return asyncio.run(run())
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'process_runtime_step_once', execute)
    getattr(contracts, case)(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    from test_worker_group import FakeProcess

def test_async_consumer_stops_claiming_and_cancels_inflight():
    from app.async_runtime_worker import consume
    async def scenario():
        stopped = threading.Event()
        live = peak = cancelled = 0
        async def step():
            nonlocal live, peak, cancelled
            live += 1
            peak = max(peak, live)
            try:
                if live == 3:
                    stopped.set()
                await asyncio.Event().wait()
            finally:
                live -= 1
                cancelled += 1
        await consume(step, slots=3, should_continue=lambda: not stopped.is_set(),
                      shutdown_seconds=.01, poll_seconds=.001)
        assert peak == 3
        assert live == 0 and cancelled == 3
    asyncio.run(scenario())


@pytest.mark.parametrize('case', [
    'test_real_prompt_provider_timeout_is_unknown_and_never_retryable',
    'test_real_prompt_client_preflight_failure_is_safe_to_retry',
    'test_real_prompt_heartbeats_parent_while_provider_is_still_running',
    'test_post_provider_database_failure_never_resends_paid_request',
    'test_real_prompt_settlement_completes_runtime_attempt_in_same_operation',
    'test_real_foreach_fail_open_closes_provider_call_when_settlement_fails',
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('split', [False, True])
def test_async_preserves_paid_failure_contracts(tmp_path, monkeypatch, case, split):
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    import test_workflow_fixture_executor as contracts
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
    from app.adaptive_admission import AdmissionGate
    def wrapper(generator):
        def execute(**kwargs):
            async def run():
                async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1,
                        phase_admission=AdmissionGate(1) if split else None) as runtime:
                    return await runtime.run(generator(**kwargs))
            return asyncio.run(run())
        return execute
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_prompt_result', wrapper(executor._real_prompt_steps))
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, '_real_foreach_result', wrapper(executor._real_foreach_steps))
    extra = {'failure_phase': 'settlement'} if case == 'test_post_provider_database_failure_never_resends_paid_request' else {}
    getattr(contracts, case)(tmp_path=tmp_path, monkeypatch=monkeypatch, **extra)


def test_deployment_exposes_bounded_async_settings():
    from pathlib import Path
    source = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'docker-compose.server.yml').read_text()
    assert '${WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS:-0}' in source
    assert '${WORKER_RUNTIME_PHASE_THREADS:-1}' in source


def test_lease_heartbeat_is_not_starved_by_image_preparation():
    from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime, ProviderWait
    async def scenario():
        provider_started = asyncio.Event()
        facts = []
        async def provider():
            provider_started.set()
            await asyncio.sleep(.04)
        def heartbeat():
            facts.append('heartbeat')
        def waiting():
            yield ProviderWait(provider, 1, heartbeat, .005)
        def preparing():
            time.sleep(.08)
            facts.append('prepared')
            if False:
                yield
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=2, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            wait = asyncio.create_task(runtime.run(waiting()))
            await provider_started.wait()
            await asyncio.gather(wait, runtime.run(preparing()))
        assert facts.index('heartbeat') < facts.index('prepared')
    asyncio.run(scenario())
