===== FILE backend/tests/test_breaker_permissions.py =====
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.database import get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import AuditEvent, CircuitBreaker, User


from test_employee_access import context


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role,allowed", [("viewer", False), ("category_manager", False), ("product_rd", False), ("manager", False), ("admin", True)])
def test_breaker_mutations_require_admin_and_audit(context, role, allowed):
    sessions, login, _ = context
    client, _ = login(role, ["space_image"])
    opened = client.post("/api/circuit-breakers/strategy/test/open", json={"reason": "maintenance", "cooldown_seconds": 60})
    reset = client.post("/api/circuit-breakers/strategy/test/reset")
    assert (opened.status_code, reset.status_code) == ((200, 200) if allowed else (403, 403))
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(CircuitBreaker)) == int(allowed)
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(AuditEvent)) == 2 * int(allowed)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("method,path", [
    ("post", "/api/loops"), ("post", "/api/material-packages"),
    ("post", "/api/prompts/ai-revise"), ("post", "/api/prompt-optimizations"),
    ("post", "/api/evaluations/1/review-panel/open"), ("post", "/api/model-benchmarks"),
    ("post", "/api/baseline-sets"), ("post", "/api/sample-sets"),
    ("patch", "/api/sample-sets/1/items/1"), ("delete", "/api/sample-sets/1/items/1"),
    ("post", "/api/prompt-regressions"), ("post", "/api/migrations"),
])
def test_viewer_cannot_mutate_legacy_entry_points(method, path):
    previous = dict(app.dependency_overrides)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: User(username="viewer", password_hash="unused", role="viewer", is_admin=False)
    def no_database():
        yield None
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = no_database
    try:
        response = TestClient(app).request(method, path, json={})
        assert response.status_code == 403, response.text
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        app.dependency_overrides.update(previous)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_candidate_rebase.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.candidate_rebase import (
    CandidateRebaseError,
    nearest_common_ancestor,
    plan_candidate_rebase,
    rebase_candidate_artifacts,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Revision


def _artifacts(contract=None, classification_map=None, subcategory_dimensions=None):
    return {
        "contract": contract or {},
        "classification_map": classification_map or {},
        "subcategory_dimensions": subcategory_dimensions or {},
    }


def test_rebase_replays_candidate_edits_onto_active_without_dropping_active_work():
    """候选自己的改动要带过来，现役版本独有的新增不能丢。

    这是变基的核心价值：分叉候选之所以不能直接启用，就是因为重放它会静默
    丢弃现役版本引入的东西。
    """
    base = _artifacts(contract={
        "redline_policy": {"enabled": False, "hit_level": "L4"},
        "spec_version": "v3",
    })
    active = _artifacts(contract={
        "redline_policy": {"enabled": False, "hit_level": "L4"},
        "spec_version": "v4",
        # 现役独有：候选那条线上从未存在
        "b_aesthetic_foundation": {"source": "call_b", "score_range": [0, 100]},
    })
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={
        "redline_policy": {"enabled": True, "hit_level": "L3"},
        "spec_version": "v3",
    })

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert result["conflicts"] == []
    merged = result["artifacts"]["contract"]
    # 候选的改动被采纳
    assert merged["redline_policy"] == {"enabled": True, "hit_level": "L3"}
    # 现役独有的块完整保留
    assert merged["b_aesthetic_foundation"] == {
        "source": "call_b",
        "score_range": [0, 100],
    }
    # 候选没碰过的字段保持现役取值
    assert merged["spec_version"] == "v4"


def test_active_only_addition_is_not_a_conflict():
    """现役新增、候选未涉及的键必须原样保留，且不得报冲突。

    手工三方合并时最易踩的坑：把「祖先无、现役有、候选无」误判为双方各自
    新增，从而虚报冲突并阻塞变基。
    """
    base = _artifacts(contract={"keep": 1})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"keep": 1, "added_by_active": {"deep": True}})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"keep": 2})

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert result["conflicts"] == []
    assert result["artifacts"]["contract"]["added_by_active"] == {"deep": True}
    assert result["artifacts"]["contract"]["keep"] == 2


def test_both_sides_changed_same_value_is_reported_as_conflict():
    base = _artifacts(contract={"hit_score_cap": 40})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"hit_score_cap": 50})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"hit_score_cap": 60})

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert [c["path"] for c in result["conflicts"]] == ["contract.hit_score_cap"]
    # 冲突时保留现役取值，绝不擅自选边
    assert result["artifacts"]["contract"]["hit_score_cap"] == 50


def test_both_sides_added_same_key_differently_is_a_conflict():
    base = _artifacts(contract={})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"foundation": {"source": "call_b"}})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"foundation": {"source": "call_a"}})

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert [c["path"] for c in result["conflicts"]] == ["contract.foundation"]


def test_identical_independent_addition_is_not_a_conflict():
    base = _artifacts(contract={})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"foundation": {"source": "call_b"}})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"foundation": {"source": "call_b"}})

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert result["conflicts"] == []
    assert result["artifacts"]["contract"]["foundation"] == {"source": "call_b"}


def test_candidate_deletion_is_adopted_but_conflicts_when_active_changed_it():
    base = _artifacts(contract={"legacy": {"a": 1}, "other": 1})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"legacy": {"a": 1}, "other": 1})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"other": 1})

    clean = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)
    assert clean["conflicts"] == []
    assert "legacy" not in clean["artifacts"]["contract"]

    active_touched = _artifacts(contract={"legacy": {"a": 2}, "other": 1})
    conflicted = rebase_candidate_artifacts(
        base=base, active=active_touched, candidate=candidate
    )
    assert [c["path"] for c in conflicted["conflicts"]] == ["contract.legacy"]


def test_lists_are_merged_atomically_not_element_wise():
    """规则数组按整体取舍：逐元素合并会把两套顺序悄悄交织在一起。"""
    base = _artifacts(contract={"rules": [{"key": "a"}]})
    active = _artifacts(contract={"rules": [{"key": "a"}]})
    candidate = _artifacts(contract={"rules": [{"key": "a"}, {"key": "b"}]})

    result = rebase_candidate_artifacts(base=base, active=active, candidate=candidate)

    assert result["conflicts"] == []
    assert result["artifacts"]["contract"]["rules"] == [{"key": "a"}, {"key": "b"}]


def _revision(db, *, revision, status, parent_id, contract, category_key="model_3d_su"):
    row = CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
        category_key=category_key,
        display_name=f"rev{revision}",
        revision=revision,
        status=status,
        parent_revision_id=parent_id,
        contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
        classification_map_json="{}",
        subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
        dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
        media_penalty_enabled=False,
        contract_hash=f"{revision:064d}",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


@pytest.fixture()
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_plan_finds_nearest_common_ancestor_across_sibling_branches(db):
    """复现真实分叉：候选与现役是兄弟分支，共同祖先在更早的版本上。"""
    root = _revision(db, revision=5, status="retired", parent_id=None,
                     contract={"spec_version": "v3", "cap": 40})
    mid = _revision(db, revision=6, status="candidate", parent_id=root.id,
                    contract={"spec_version": "v3", "cap": 40, "extra": 1})
    candidate = _revision(db, revision=7, status="candidate", parent_id=mid.id,
                          contract={"spec_version": "v3", "cap": 60, "extra": 1})
    active = _revision(db, revision=8, status="active", parent_id=root.id,
                       contract={"spec_version": "v4", "cap": 40, "foundation": True})

    assert nearest_common_ancestor(db, candidate, active).id == root.id

    plan = plan_candidate_rebase(db, candidate=candidate, active=active)
    assert plan["needed"] is True
    assert plan["base_revision_id"] == root.id
    assert plan["conflicts"] == []
    merged = plan["artifacts"]["contract"]
    assert merged["cap"] == 60           # 候选改动
    assert merged["extra"] == 1          # 中间版本引入、候选继承
    assert merged["foundation"] is True  # 现役独有，保留
    assert merged["spec_version"] == "v4"


def test_plan_reports_no_rebase_needed_for_direct_child(db):
    active = _revision(db, revision=8, status="active", parent_id=None, contract={"a": 1})
    candidate = _revision(db, revision=9, status="candidate", parent_id=active.id,
                          contract={"a": 2})

    plan = plan_candidate_rebase(db, candidate=candidate, active=active)

    assert plan["needed"] is False
    assert plan["conflicts"] == []


def test_plan_rejects_non_candidate_and_foreign_category(db):
    active = _revision(db, revision=8, status="active", parent_id=None, contract={})
    retired = _revision(db, revision=6, status="retired", parent_id=None, contract={})
    foreign = _revision(db, revision=3, status="candidate", parent_id=None, contract={},
                        category_key="inspiration_image")

    with pytest.raises(CandidateRebaseError) as retired_error:
        plan_candidate_rebase(db, candidate=retired, active=active)
    assert retired_error.value.code == "candidate_status_conflict"

    with pytest.raises(CandidateRebaseError) as foreign_error:
        plan_candidate_rebase(db, candidate=foreign, active=active)
    assert foreign_error.value.code == "candidate_category_conflict"


def test_plan_rejects_candidate_without_shared_history(db):
    active = _revision(db, revision=8, status="active", parent_id=None, contract={})
    orphan = _revision(db, revision=4, status="candidate", parent_id=None, contract={})

    with pytest.raises(CandidateRebaseError) as exc_info:
        plan_candidate_rebase(db, candidate=orphan, active=active)
    assert exc_info.value.code == "no_common_ancestor"



===== FILE backend/tests/test_capacity_stream.py =====
"""Catch burst-only admission and reporting that conceals a growing backlog."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker


def module():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/capacity_stream.py'
    assert path.exists(), 'continuous demand probe missing'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('stream_probe', path)
    result = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(result)
    return result


def test_feed_holds_future_work_and_releases_only_one_bounded_tick():
    m = module()
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with engine.begin() as db:
        db.execute(text('CREATE TABLE runtime_dispatch_items (id INTEGER, status TEXT, available_at DATETIME)'))
        db.execute(text("INSERT INTO runtime_dispatch_items VALUES (1,'queued',NULL),(2,'queued',NULL),(3,'queued',NULL)"))
    feed = m.DemandFeed(sessionmaker(bind=engine), items=3, rate=2)
    feed.hold()
    with engine.connect() as db:
        assert db.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM runtime_dispatch_items WHERE available_at > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP')).scalar() == 3
    assert feed.release_tick() == 2
    with engine.connect() as db:
        assert db.execute(text('SELECT count(*) FROM runtime_dispatch_items WHERE available_at > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP')).scalar() == 1
    assert feed.release_tick() == 1
    assert feed.release_tick() == 0


def test_summary_distinguishes_completion_from_sustainable_input_rate():
    m = module()
    rows = [dict(elapsed=t, admitted=a, succeeded=s, pending=a-s) for t,a,s in
            [(0,0,0),(30,360,200),(60,720,400),(90,1080,600),(120,1440,800),(200,1440,1440)]]
    r = m.summarize(rows, items=1440, rate=12, duration=120)
    assert r['drained']
    assert r['steady_completion_per_second'] == pytest.approx(600/90)
    assert r['backlog_growth_per_second'] == pytest.approx(480/90)
    assert not r['sustained_rate_met']


def test_partial_feed_or_missing_steady_samples_cannot_pass():
    m = module()
    for rows in [[], [dict(elapsed=0,admitted=0,succeeded=0,pending=0),
                      dict(elapsed=120,admitted=5,succeeded=5,pending=0)]]:
        assert not m.summarize(rows, items=1440, rate=12, duration=120)['sustained_rate_met']

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_dimension_baseline_http.py =====
"""Exercise the public import-to-baseline boundary with real synthetic images."""
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func

import app.main as main_module
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, DesignCaseSample
from test_design_case_sample_api import _client
from test_design_case_dimension_pipeline import CASE_TEXT, _png_bytes


def import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    root = tmp_path / 'nas'
    directory = root / 'delivery' / '02中等' / '中等_1'
    directory.mkdir(parents=True)
    (directory / '1.txt').write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding='utf8')
    (directory / '好_101.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    (directory / '中等_102.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    original_settings = main_module.get_settings()
    monkeypatch.setattr(main_module, 'get_settings', lambda: SimpleNamespace(**{**original_settings.__dict__, 'nas_maps_root': root}))
    response = client.post('/api/design-case-dimension-samples/import', json={'root_uri':'nas://maps/delivery','category_key':'design_case','package_name':'synthetic dimension'})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()['package']['id']


def create_set(client, package):
    return client.post('/api/baseline-sets', json={
        'name': 'dimension HTTP regression', 'category_key':'design_case',
        'source_package_id': package, 'package_truth_contract_key':'design_case.dimension_grade.v1',
        'items': [],
    })


def test_imported_dimension_package_freezes_shared_asset_identities_via_http(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        response = create_set(client, package)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        data = response.json()
        assert data['item_count'] == 1
        detail = client.get(f"/api/baseline-sets/{data['id']}")
        assert detail.status_code == 200, detail.text
        item = detail.json()['items'][0]
        assert item['truth_contract_key'] == 'design_case.dimension_grade.v1'
        assert item['expected_result']['images'] == [{'cloud_id':'101','level':'L1'},{'cloud_id':'102','level':'L2'}]
        images = item['workflow_input']['case']['images']
        assert [image['cloud_id'] for image in images] == ['101','102']
        assert len({image['asset_id'] for image in images}) == 1
        assert set(item['workflow_input']['case']).isdisjoint({'quality_score','time_score','filter_threshold'})


@pytest.mark.parametrize('drift,status,code', [
    ('deleted',404,'design_case_image_asset_missing'),
    ('category',409,'design_case_image_category_mismatch'),
    ('hash',409,'design_case_image_hash_mismatch'),
    ('mime',422,'design_case_image_type_invalid'),
])
def test_dimension_baseline_rejects_invalid_image_assets_before_writes(tmp_path, monkeypatch, drift, status, code):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        with sessions() as db:
            sample = db.scalar(select(DesignCaseSample))
            image_id = json.loads(sample.image_grades_json)['images'][0]['asset_id']
            asset = db.get(Asset,image_id)
            if drift == 'deleted': asset.status = 'deleted'
            if drift == 'category': asset.category_key = 'space_image'
            if drift == 'hash': asset.sha256 = 'b' * 64
            if drift == 'mime': asset.mime_type = 'application/pdf'
            db.commit()
        response = create_set(client, package)
        assert response.status_code == status, response.text
        assert response.json()['detail']['code'] == code
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineSet)) == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field', ['case_cloud_id', 'image_cloud_id'])
def test_dimension_manual_request_cannot_reassign_imported_identity(tmp_path, monkeypatch, field):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        with sessions() as db:
            sample = db.scalar(select(DesignCaseSample))
            grades = json.loads(sample.image_grades_json)
            case = json.loads(sample.case_json)
            case['images'] = [{k: image[k] for k in ('cloud_id','asset_id','asset_sha256')} for image in grades['images']]
            truth = {'level':grades['case_level'],'images':[{k: image[k] for k in ('cloud_id','level')} for image in grades['images']]}
            if field == 'case_cloud_id': case['cloud_id'] = 'other-case'
            else:
                case['images'][0]['cloud_id'] = truth['images'][0]['cloud_id'] = 'other-image'
            asset_id = sample.asset_id
        response = client.post('/api/baseline-sets', json={
            'name':'forged identity','category_key':'design_case','items':[{
                'asset_id':asset_id,'source_package_id':package,
                'truth_contract_key':'design_case.dimension_grade.v1',
                'workflow_input':{'case':case},'expected_result':truth,
            }],
        })
        assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
        assert response.json()['detail']['code'] == 'design_case_dimension_identity_mismatch'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_dimension_multiline_text.py =====
"""Delivery text must keep full prose while splitting only registered fields."""
from app.design_case_dimension_import import _read_text_fields, parse_case_directory
from app.design_case_dimension_service import _case_payload


def test_multiline_body_preserves_paragraphs_and_unregistered_colon_headings(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'case.txt'
    path.write_text(
        '素材标题：山地建筑\n'
        '案例正文：第一段。\n'
        '第二行继续正文。\n\n'
        '创作思想：顺应山势。\n'
        '  保留正文缩进。\n'
        'https://example.invalid/reference\n'
        '城市：中国\n'
        '设计亮点/设计说明：坡地设计\n'
        '保留原有树木。\n'
        '所有图片的类型：实景照片有1张\n', encoding='utf8')
    fields = _read_text_fields(path)
    assert fields['案例正文'] == (
        '第一段。\n第二行继续正文。\n\n创作思想：顺应山势。\n'
        '  保留正文缩进。\nhttps://example.invalid/reference')
    assert fields['城市'] == '中国'
    assert fields['设计亮点/设计说明'] == '坡地设计\n保留原有树木。'
    assert '创作思想' not in fields


def test_repeated_field_discards_its_whole_block_without_polluting_first_value(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'case.txt'
    path.write_text(
        '案例正文：第一版\n第一版续行\n'
        '案例正文：重复版\n重复版续行\n创作思想：重复版细节\n'
        '城市：中国\n', encoding='utf8')
    assert _read_text_fields(path) == {'案例正文': '第一版\n第一版续行', '城市': '中国'}


def test_empty_header_multiline_body_reaches_case_payload(tmp_path):
    case_dir = tmp_path / '好_1'
    case_dir.mkdir()
    (case_dir / '1.txt').write_text(
        '素材标题：测试\n案例正文：\n完整正文首段。\n完整正文末段。\n'
        '图片总张数：1张\n所有图片的类型：实景照片有1张\n', encoding='utf8')
    (case_dir / '好_101.png').write_bytes(b'parser does not decode images')
    parsed = parse_case_directory(case_dir, grade='好')
    payload = _case_payload(parsed)
    assert payload['case_text'] == '完整正文首段。\n完整正文末段。'
    assert parsed.text_fields['图片总张数'] == '1张'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_dimension_package_membership.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.main import _design_case_aggregate_items, _design_case_dimension_items
from app.models import MaterialPackage
from test_design_case_dimension_persistence import db, delivery, load_plan, save


@pytest.mark.parametrize("selector", [_design_case_aggregate_items, _design_case_dimension_items])
def test_mixed_delivery_baseline_includes_reused_case(db, delivery, selector):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    first = save(db, plans)
    new = deepcopy(plans[0])
    new.case["cloud_id"] = new.cloud_id = "2"
    second = save(db, [plans[0], new])
    package = db.get(MaterialPackage, second["package"]["id"])
    selected = selector(db, package)
    assert len(selected) == 2
    assert first["items"][0]["asset_id"] in {item.asset_id for item, _ in selected}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_dimension_shared_assets.py =====
"""Delivery IDs remain independent even when their image bytes are identical."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from app.baseline_truth import (
    BaselineTruthError, DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
    DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT, normalize_baseline_workflow_input,
    normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result,
)
from app.models import Asset, DesignCaseSample
from app.design_case_dimension_service import build_import_plan
from app.workflow_foreach_executor import _desired_items, _invocation_key
from app.workflow_case_aggregation import aggregate_case
from app.design_case_image_grade import build_image_grade_projection, compare_image_grades
from test_design_case_dimension_persistence import db, delivery, load_plan, save
from test_design_case_dimension_pipeline import CASE_TEXT, _png_bytes


def freeze(images):
    return normalize_baseline_workflow_input(
        {'case': {'cloud_id': '1', 'case_name': 'case', 'images': images}},
        truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
    )


def test_shared_bytes_keep_independent_truth_foreach_items_and_comparisons(db, delivery):
    (delivery[1] / '中等_102.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    result = save(db, plans)
    sample = db.get(DesignCaseSample, result['items'][0]['sample_id'])
    grades = json.loads(sample.image_grades_json)
    images = grades['images']
    assert len(images) == 2
    assert len({image['asset_id'] for image in images}) == 1
    assert len({image['source_uri'] for image in images}) == 2
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Asset).where(Asset.storage_backend == 'nas_maps')) == 1
    frozen = freeze([{key: image[key] for key in ('cloud_id', 'asset_id', 'asset_sha256')} for image in images])
    truth = normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result(
        {'level': grades['case_level'], 'images': [{key: image[key] for key in ('cloud_id', 'level')} for image in images]},
        workflow_input=frozen,
    )
    node = {'kind': 'foreach_image', 'config': {
        'items_path': '$.case.images', 'item_identity_path': '$.cloud_id',
        'max_items': 10, 'item_executor': {'kind': 'prompt'},
    }}
    desired, source_hash, contract_hash = _desired_items(node, frozen)
    assert [item[0] for item in desired] == ['101', '102']
    assert len({item[3] for item in desired}) == 2
    assert len({_invocation_key(item[0], 1) for item in desired}) == 2
    # Local synthetic responses: each independent invocation scores the same bytes.
    source = {
        'schema_version': 'workflow-foreach-output-v1', 'node_instance_id': 'foreach',
        'source_hash': source_hash, 'item_contract_hash': contract_hash,
        'counts': {'total': 2, 'succeeded': 2, 'failed': 0, 'skipped': 0},
        'items': [
            {'item_key': key, 'ordinal': ordinal, 'status': 'succeeded',
             'attempt_count': 1, 'input_hash': digest, 'output_hash': 'fixture', 'output': {'score': 88}}
            for key, ordinal, _, digest in desired
        ],
    }
    aggregate = aggregate_case({'upstream': {'foreach': source}}, {
        'foreach_node_id': 'foreach', 'included_statuses': ['succeeded'],
        'score_path': '$.score', 'feature_paths': {},
        'empty_policy': 'fail', 'failure_projection': 'include',
    })
    projected = build_image_grade_projection(aggregate)
    comparison = compare_image_grades(truth['images'], projected['images'])
    assert comparison['compared'] == 2
    assert comparison['correct'] == 1
    assert comparison['over_graded'] == 1
    assert comparison['missing'] == []
    assert truth['images'] == [{'cloud_id': '101', 'level': 'L1'}, {'cloud_id': '102', 'level': 'L2'}]


def test_distinct_cloud_ids_in_different_cases_can_share_bytes(delivery):
    second = delivery[0] / 'delivery' / '03中差' / '中差_2'
    second.mkdir(parents=True)
    (second / '2.txt').write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding='utf8')
    (second / '中差_202.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    plans, stats = build_import_plan('nas://maps/delivery', nas_root=delivery[0])
    assert stats['cases'] == 2 and stats['images'] == 2
    assert {image.cloud_id for plan in plans for image in plan.images} == {'101', '202'}


def test_shared_asset_with_conflicting_frozen_hash_is_rejected():
    images = [
        {'cloud_id': '101', 'asset_id': 1, 'asset_sha256': 'a' * 64},
        {'cloud_id': '102', 'asset_id': 1, 'asset_sha256': 'b' * 64},
    ]
    with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as error:
        freeze(images)
    assert error.value.code == 'design_case_dimension_asset_hash_conflict'


def test_shared_asset_does_not_allow_duplicate_cloud_ids():
    image = {'cloud_id': '101', 'asset_id': 1, 'asset_sha256': 'a' * 64}
    with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as error:
        freeze([image, deepcopy(image)])
    assert error.value.code == 'design_case_dimension_input_duplicate'


def test_legacy_review_still_rejects_shared_asset_ids():
    case = {
        'cloud_id': 'case-1', 'project_id': 'project-1', 'case_name': 'case',
        'case_text': 'description', 'quality_score': 80, 'time_score': 70,
        'filter_threshold': 60,
        'images': [
            {'cloud_id': key, 'asset_id': 1, 'asset_sha256': 'a' * 64,
             'oss_url': f'https://example.invalid/{key}.jpg'}
            for key in ('101', '102')
        ],
    }
    with pytest.raises(BaselineTruthError) as error:
        normalize_baseline_workflow_input({'case': case}, truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT)
    assert error.value.code == 'baseline_truth_design_case_image_duplicate'


def test_same_cloud_id_across_cases_rejected_even_when_content_differs(delivery):
    from app.design_case_dimension_import import DesignCaseDimensionError

    second = delivery[0] / 'delivery' / '03中差' / '中差_2'
    second.mkdir(parents=True)
    (second / '2.txt').write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding='utf8')
    (second / '中差_101.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes() + b'different image bytes')
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as error:
        load_plan(delivery)
    assert error.value.code == 'design_case_image_cross_case_duplicate'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_dimension_truth_revision_http.py =====
"""The public image-truth editor appends revisions without changing frozen inputs."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.baseline_truth import resolve_effective_baseline_truth
from app.models import BaselineSetItem, BaselineTruthRevision, DesignCaseSample
from app.native_baseline_regression import _production_input_manifest, compute_truth_metrics
from app.workflow_model_executor import _redact_model_context
from test_case_dimension_baseline_http import import_package, create_set
from test_design_case_sample_api import _client


def item_snapshot(db, item_id):
    item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, item_id)
    return item.expected_result_json, item.asset_snapshot_json


def test_dimension_http_revision_is_idempotent_and_used_by_next_native_input(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        created = create_set(client, package)
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        set_id = created.json()['id']
        original = client.get(f'/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}').json()['items'][0]
        item_id = original['id']
        with sessions() as db:
            before = item_snapshot(db, item_id)
            source_grades = db.scalar(select(DesignCaseSample)).image_grades_json
            item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, item_id)
            prior_input = deepcopy(_production_input_manifest(item, resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item)))
        corrected = deepcopy(original['expected_result'])
        corrected['images'][0]['level'] = 'L4'
        payload = {'edit_key': 'synthetic-review', 'reason': 'synthetic human review',
                   'truth_contract_key': original['truth_contract_key'], 'expected_result': corrected}
        url = f'/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item_id}/truth'
        response = client.post(url, json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        edited = response.json()
        assert edited['truth_revision'] == 1
        assert edited['expected_result'] == corrected
        assert edited['original_truth'] == original['original_truth']
        assert edited['workflow_input'] == original['workflow_input']
        replay = client.post(url, json=payload)
        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert replay.json()['truth_revision_id'] == edited['truth_revision_id']
        conflicting = deepcopy(payload)
        conflicting['expected_result']['images'][1]['level'] = 'L3'
        conflict = client.post(url, json=conflicting)
        assert conflict.status_code == 409, conflict.text
        assert conflict.json()['detail']['code'] == 'truth_revision_idempotency_conflict'
        latest = client.get(f'/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}').json()['items'][0]
        assert latest['expected_result'] == corrected
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineTruthRevision)) == 1
            assert item_snapshot(db, item_id) == before
            assert db.scalar(select(DesignCaseSample)).image_grades_json == source_grades
            item = db.get(BaselineSetItem, item_id)
            next_input = _production_input_manifest(item, resolve_effective_baseline_truth(db, item))
        assert next_input['case'] == prior_input['case']
        assert prior_input['expected_result'] == original['expected_result']
        assert next_input['expected_result'] == corrected
        assert next_input['truth_revision'] == 1
        assert 'expected_result' not in _redact_model_context(next_input)
        prediction = {'level': 'L2', 'images': [{'cloud_id': key, 'level': 'L1'} for key in ('101', '102')]}
        def metrics(expected):
            return compute_truth_metrics([{'status':'completed', 'expected_result':expected, 'predicted_result':prediction}])['image_grade_metrics']
        assert metrics(prior_input['expected_result'])['correct'] == 1
        assert metrics(next_input['expected_result'])['correct'] == 0
        assert metrics(next_input['expected_result'])['compared'] == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize('invalid', ['missing_image', 'extra_image', 'image_l5', 'input_identity'])
def test_dimension_truth_edit_rejects_incomplete_truth_and_input_changes(tmp_path, monkeypatch, invalid):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        set_id = create_set(client, package).json()['id']
        original = client.get(f'/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}').json()['items'][0]
        payload = {'edit_key':'invalid-review', 'reason':'synthetic review',
                   'truth_contract_key':original['truth_contract_key'],
                   'expected_result':deepcopy(original['expected_result'])}
        expected_status = 422
        if invalid == 'missing_image': payload['expected_result']['images'].pop()
        if invalid == 'extra_image': payload['expected_result']['images'].append({'cloud_id':'999','level':'L1'})
        if invalid == 'image_l5': payload['expected_result']['images'][0]['level'] = 'L5'
        if invalid == 'input_identity':
            payload['workflow_input'] = deepcopy(original['workflow_input'])
            payload['workflow_input']['case']['cloud_id'] = 'different-case'
            expected_status = 409
        response = client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{original['id']}/truth", json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == expected_status, response.text
        assert response.json()['detail']['code'] != 'kg_output_contract_unknown'
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineTruthRevision)) == 0


def test_dimension_truth_edit_cannot_change_another_contract(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    with _client() as (client, sessions):
        package = import_package(client, tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        created = client.post('/api/baseline-sets', json={
            'name':'synthetic aggregate', 'category_key':'design_case',
            'source_package_id':package,
            'package_truth_contract_key':'design_case.material_aggregate.v1', 'items':[],
        })
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        set_id = created.json()['id']
        item = client.get(f'/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}').json()['items'][0]
        response = client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/items/{item['id']}/truth", json={
            'edit_key':'wrong-contract', 'reason':'synthetic review',
            'truth_contract_key':'design_case.dimension_grade.v1',
            'expected_result':{'level':'L2','images':[{'cloud_id':'101','level':'L1'}]},
        })
        assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
        assert response.json()['detail']['code'] == 'baseline_truth_contract_mismatch'
        with sessions() as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineTruthRevision)) == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_grade_freeze.py =====
"""New workflow versions freeze image grade defaults without altering drafts."""
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import ModelConfig, ModelRegistryEntry
from app.workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references, validate_managed_workflow_v3
from test_workflow_v3 import _node
from test_workflow_working_copies import _engine, _seed_managed_dependencies


@pytest.fixture
def managed_case(tmp_path):
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt = next(node for node in manifest['nodes'] if node['kind'] == 'prompt')
            executor = deepcopy(prompt['config'])
            executor.update({
                'kind': 'prompt', 'input_modality': 'image',
                'thinking_level': 'none', 'thinking_enabled': False,
                'structured_output': True,
                'output_contract': {
                    'mode': 'json_schema', 'name': 'case_image_result',
                    'description': '案例单图结果', 'strict': True,
                    'schema': {
                        'type': 'object', 'properties': {'score': {'type': 'number'}},
                        'required': ['score'], 'additionalProperties': False,
                    },
                },
            })
            model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, executor['model_registry_id'])
            source = db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
            model.protocol = source.protocol = 'openai_chat'
            prompt['kind'] = 'foreach_image'
            prompt['config'] = {
                'items_path': '$.case.images', 'item_identity_path': '$.cloud_id',
                'max_items': 1000, 'max_parallel_items': 4,
                'failure_policy': 'fail_open', 'item_executor': executor,
            }
            aggregate = _node('aggregate', 'aggregate_case', {
                'foreach_node_id': prompt['node_instance_id'],
                'included_statuses': ['succeeded', 'failed', 'skipped'],
                'score_path': '$.score', 'feature_paths': {},
                'score_buckets': [{'label': 'all', 'min_inclusive': 0, 'max_exclusive': 101}],
                'empty_policy': 'fail',
                'failure_projection': 'exclude', 'emit_image_grades': True,
            })
            manifest['nodes'].insert(2, aggregate)
            edge = next(edge for edge in manifest['edges'] if edge['from'] == prompt['node_instance_id'])
            target = edge['to']
            edge['to'] = 'aggregate'
            manifest['edges'].append({'from': 'aggregate', 'to': target})
            assert validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, manifest) == []
            yield db, manifest
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def aggregate_config(manifest):
    return next(node['config'] for node in manifest['nodes'] if node['kind'] == 'aggregate_case')


@pytest.mark.parametrize('explicit_null', [False, True])
def test_freeze_writes_default_bands_without_mutating_input(managed_case, explicit_null):
    db, manifest = managed_case
    if explicit_null:
        aggregate_config(manifest)['image_grade_bands'] = None
    before = deepcopy(manifest)
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    assert aggregate_config(frozen)['image_grade_bands'] == [
        {'level': 'L1', 'min_inclusive': 85.0},
        {'level': 'L2', 'min_inclusive': 72.0},
        {'level': 'L3', 'min_inclusive': 60.0},
        {'level': 'L4', 'min_inclusive': 0.0},
    ]
    assert manifest == before


def test_freeze_preserves_explicit_bands_as_independent_copy(managed_case):
    db, manifest = managed_case
    custom = [
        {'level': level, 'min_inclusive': minimum}
        for level, minimum in [('L1', 92), ('L2', 81), ('L3', 67), ('L4', 0)]
    ]
    aggregate_config(manifest)['image_grade_bands'] = custom
    before = deepcopy(manifest)
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    assert aggregate_config(frozen)['image_grade_bands'] == custom
    aggregate_config(frozen)['image_grade_bands'][0]['min_inclusive'] = 90
    assert manifest == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('enabled', [False, None])
def test_freeze_does_not_add_bands_when_projection_is_disabled(managed_case, enabled):
    db, manifest = managed_case
    if enabled is None:
        aggregate_config(manifest).pop('emit_image_grades')
    else:
        aggregate_config(manifest)['emit_image_grades'] = enabled
    before = deepcopy(manifest)
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    assert 'image_grade_bands' not in aggregate_config(frozen)
    assert manifest == before

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_image_grades_mysql84_acceptance.py =====
"""Opt-in minimal-schema acceptance on an isolated local Oracle MySQL 8.4.

Run with CASE_MIGRATION_MYSQL84_URL pointing at a disposable local MySQL
instance's mysql maintenance database, then pytest this file. No application
DB, model calls, full metadata initialization, or remote connections are used.
Only a freshly generated case_migration_test_<uuid> database is created/dropped.
"""
import os
import uuid

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, inspect
from sqlalchemy.engine import make_url

from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    MySQLBaselineError,
    assert_mysql_schema_version,
    upgrade_mysql_schema,
)


@pytest.fixture
def isolated_mysql84():
    configured = os.environ.get('CASE_MIGRATION_MYSQL84_URL')
    if not configured:
        pytest.skip('CASE_MIGRATION_MYSQL84_URL is unset; local MySQL 8.4 acceptance is opt-in')
    url = make_url(configured)
    assert url.get_backend_name() == 'mysql'
    assert url.host in {'127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '::1'}, 'Only an isolated local instance is allowed'
    assert url.database == 'mysql', 'Use the mysql maintenance DB, never an application database'
    assert not url.query, 'Connection routing options are not accepted'
    name = 'case_migration_test_' + uuid.uuid4().hex
    admin = create_engine(url, isolation_level='AUTOCOMMIT')
    engine = None
    created = False
    try:
        with admin.connect() as connection:
            assert str(connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT VERSION()').scalar_one()).startswith('8.4.')
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                f'CREATE DATABASE `{name}` CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs'
            )
            created = True
        engine = create_engine(url.set(database=name), isolation_level='READ COMMITTED')
        event.listen(engine, 'connect', lambda dbapi, record: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(dbapi))
        yield engine
    finally:
        if engine is not None:
            engine.dispose()
        if created:
            with admin.connect() as connection:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(f'DROP DATABASE `{name}`')
        admin.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('initial', ['missing', 'interrupted_add', 'legacy_not_null', 'legacy_nullable'])
def test_mysql84_upgrade_and_retry(isolated_mysql84, initial):
    engine = isolated_mysql84
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name VARCHAR(200))')
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (111, 'fixture_111')")
        connection.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE design_case_samples (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')
        connection.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples VALUES (1), (2)')
        if initial in {'legacy_not_null', 'legacy_nullable'}:
            nullable = 'NULL' if initial == 'legacy_nullable' else 'NOT NULL'
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f'ALTER TABLE design_case_samples ADD COLUMN image_grades_json TEXT {nullable}')
            connection.exec_driver_sql("UPDATE design_case_samples SET image_grades_json = '' WHERE id = 1")
            connection.exec_driver_sql('UPDATE design_case_samples SET image_grades_json = %s WHERE id = 2', ('{"view":"A"}',))

        if initial == 'legacy_nullable':
            connection.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples (id, image_grades_json) VALUES (4, NULL)')

    if initial == 'interrupted_add':
        def disconnect_after_add(connection, cursor, statement, parameters, context, executemany):
            if 'ADD COLUMN `image_grades_json`' in statement:
                raise RuntimeError('disconnect after ADD DDL')
        event.listen(engine, 'after_cursor_execute', disconnect_after_add)
        try:
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='disconnect after ADD'):
                with engine.begin() as connection:
                    upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        finally:
            event.remove(engine, 'after_cursor_execute', disconnect_after_add)
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations').scalar_one() == 111
            assert 'image_grades_json' in {column['name'] for column in inspect(connection).get_columns('design_case_samples')}

    with engine.begin() as connection:
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=112)
        rows = connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT image_grades_json FROM design_case_samples ORDER BY id').scalars().all()
        expected_rows = ['{}', '{"view":"A"}'] if initial.startswith('legacy') else ['{}', '{}']
        if initial == 'legacy_nullable':
            expected_rows.append('{}')
        assert rows == expected_rows
        connection.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples (id) VALUES (3)')
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT image_grades_json FROM design_case_samples WHERE id=3').scalar_one() == '{}'
        column = next(c for c in inspect(connection).get_columns('design_case_samples') if c['name'] == 'image_grades_json')
        assert column['nullable'] is False
        assert column['default'] is not None
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM schema_migrations WHERE version=112').scalar_one() == 1
        with pytest.raises(MySQLBaselineError):
            assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=111)
        with pytest.raises(MySQLBaselineError):
            upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=111)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_case_image_grades_mysql_migration.py =====
"""111 → 112 recovery contract; local tests execute backfill against SQLite."""
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect

from app.migrations import mysql_baseline as migration


class MigrationConnection:
    """Translate MySQL DDL only; run data changes and ledger on a real database."""

    def __init__(self, connection, interrupt_after_add=False):
        self.connection = connection
        self.ddl = []
        self.interrupt_after_add = interrupt_after_add

    def exec_driver_sql(self, statement, parameters=()):
        if statement.startswith('ALTER TABLE'):
            self.ddl.append(statement)
            if 'ADD COLUMN' in statement:
                result = self.connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    'ALTER TABLE design_case_samples ADD COLUMN image_grades_json TEXT'
                )
                if self.interrupt_after_add:
                    self.interrupt_after_add = False
                    raise RuntimeError('simulated disconnect after committed ADD')
                return result
            return None
        return self.connection.exec_driver_sql(statement.replace('%s', '?'), parameters)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('existing', [False, True])
def test_backfill_preserves_grades_and_installs_default(monkeypatch, existing):
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with engine.begin() as db:
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)')
        db.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (111, 'existing')")
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE design_case_samples (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY' +
                           (', image_grades_json TEXT)' if existing else ')'))
        if existing:
            db.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples VALUES (?, ?)',
                               [(1, None), (2, ''), (3, '{"view":"A"}')])
        else:
            db.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples VALUES (1), (2)')
        connection = MigrationConnection(db)
        monkeypatch.setattr(migration, '_require_mysql', lambda c: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(migration, 'inspect', lambda c: inspect(c.connection))
        migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        values = db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT image_grades_json FROM design_case_samples ORDER BY id').scalars().all()
        assert values == (['{}', '{}', '{"view":"A"}'] if existing else ['{}', '{}'])
        assert any("TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ('{}')" in sql for sql in connection.ddl)
        before = list(connection.ddl)
        migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        assert connection.ddl == before
        assert db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM schema_migrations WHERE version=112').scalar_one() == 1
        with pytest.raises(migration.MySQLBaselineError):
            migration.assert_mysql_schema_version(connection, expected=111)
        with pytest.raises(migration.MySQLBaselineError):
            migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=111)
    engine.dispose()


def test_resume_after_add_ddl_commits(monkeypatch):
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    with engine.begin() as db:
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT)')
        db.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (111, 'existing')")
        db.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE design_case_samples (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')
        db.exec_driver_sql('INSERT INTO design_case_samples VALUES (1)')
        connection = MigrationConnection(db, interrupt_after_add=True)
        monkeypatch.setattr(migration, '_require_mysql', lambda c: None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(migration, 'inspect', lambda c: inspect(c.connection))
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='simulated disconnect'):
            migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        assert db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations').scalar_one() == 111
        migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=112)
        assert db.exec_driver_sql('SELECT image_grades_json FROM design_case_samples').scalar_one() == '{}'
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_category_evaluation_aggregator.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.category_evaluation_aggregator import (
    AGGREGATOR_VERSION,
    LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION,
    CategoryEvaluationAggregatorError,
    aggregate_category_evaluation,
)
from app.category_evaluation_contract import CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION
from app.redline_policy import REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION


# --- Inspiration-image fixtures: three tracks 一类40+60=100 / 二类20+60=80 /
# --- 三类40+30=70, media 实拍0/效果图-5/AI-15, veto 80→79. ---


def _redline_policy() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION,
        "enabled": True,
        "hit_level": "L5",
        "hit_score_cap": 49,
        "rules": [
            {
                "key": "screenshot",
                "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                "match_any": ["是截图"],
                "exemptions": [],
            }
        ],
    }


def _four_level_scale() -> dict:
    return {
        "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
        "levels": [
            {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
            {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
            {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
            {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
            {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
        ],
    }


def test_four_level_category_never_emits_disabled_l5() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["level_scale"] = _four_level_scale()
    contract["redline_policy"]["hit_level"] = "L4"
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract,
        _precheck(trait="AI图"),
        {"deductions": {"composition": 60}},
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert result["level_scale"]["enabled_levels"] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]
    assert result["level_scale"]["disabled_levels"] == ["L5"]


def test_four_level_redline_terminates_as_l4_with_hard_reject_evidence() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["level_scale"] = _four_level_scale()
    contract["redline_policy"]["hit_level"] = "L4"
    precheck = _precheck()
    precheck["production_fields"]["reason"] = ["是截图"]
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, precheck, _dimensions(), track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert result["hard_reject"] is True
    assert result["level"] == "L4"
    assert result["terminated_at"] == "redline"


def _track_classification() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
        "tracks": [
            {
                "key": "class_one",
                "label": "一类",
                "base_score": 40,
                "dimension_max": 60,
                "track_cap": 100,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "space_aesthetic", "version": "1.3.0"},
            },
            {
                "key": "class_two",
                "label": "二类",
                "base_score": 20,
                "dimension_max": 60,
                "track_cap": 80,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "space_aesthetic", "version": "1.3.0"},
            },
            {
                "key": "class_three",
                "label": "三类",
                "base_score": 40,
                "dimension_max": 30,
                "track_cap": 70,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "space_aesthetic", "version": "1.3.0"},
            },
        ],
        "default_track": "class_three",
    }


def _common_modifiers() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": "common-modifiers-v1",
        "media_type_penalty": {
            "baseline": "real_photo",
            "penalties": {"real_photo": 0, "render_3d": -5, "ai_image": -15, "other": 0},
        },
        "high_score_veto": {"threshold": 80, "cap_to": 79},
    }


def _contract() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        "redline_policy": _redline_policy(),
        "track_classification": _track_classification(),
        "common_modifiers": _common_modifiers(),
    }


def _precheck(*, trait: str = "实景照片", reason=None, hard_defects=None) -> dict:
    precheck: dict = {"production_fields": {"trait": trait, "reason": list(reason or [])}}
    if hard_defects is not None:
        precheck["hard_defects"] = hard_defects
    return precheck


def _dimensions(deductions=None, evidence=None) -> dict:
    result: dict = {"deductions": dict(deductions or {})}
    if evidence is not None:
        result["evidence"] = evidence
    return result


def _step_score(result: dict, step: str):
    for entry in result["steps"]:
        if entry["step"] == step:
            return entry["score_after"]
    raise AssertionError(f"step {step} not recorded")


# --- Redline: direct L5 + hard_reject + score capped at 49, no scoring steps. ---


def test_redline_hit_returns_l5_hard_reject_capped_and_terminates() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片", reason=["是截图"]),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["hard_reject"] is True
    assert result["terminated_at"] == "redline"
    assert result["level"] == "L5"
    assert result["raw_level"] == "L5"
    assert result["score"] == 49
    assert result["hit_rules"] == ["screenshot"]
    assert result["track_key"] is None
    # No dimension/track/media/veto step ran — only the redline step.
    recorded = {entry["step"] for entry in result["steps"]}
    assert recorded == {"redline"}
    assert any(cap["cap"] == "redline" for cap in result["caps"])


# --- Class-one perfect chain (no deductions, real photo): 40+60=100 → L1. ---


def test_class_one_full_score_real_photo_is_l1() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片"),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["hard_reject"] is False
    assert result["terminated_at"] is None
    assert result["track_key"] == "class_one"
    assert result["base_score"] == 40
    assert result["dimension_max"] == 60
    assert result["score"] == 100
    assert result["level"] == "L1"
    assert result["raw_level"] == "L1"
    assert _step_score(result, "media") == 100.0


# --- AI-image penalty: 100 - 15 = 85 → still L1, media evidence recorded. ---


def test_ai_image_penalty_keeps_l1_and_records_media_evidence() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="AI图"),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 85
    assert result["level"] == "L1"
    media_note = next(s["note"] for s in result["steps"] if s["step"] == "media")
    assert "ai_image" in media_note
    assert "-15" in media_note
    assert _step_score(result, "media") == 85.0


def test_render_3d_penalty_is_minus_five() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="3D数字效果图"),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 95
    assert result["level"] == "L1"


# --- Dimension deductions cumulative clamp to dimension_max. ---


def test_dimension_deductions_clamp_to_dimension_max() -> None:
    # 一类 dimension_max=60; ask for 100 total deduction — clamps to 60.
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片"),
        _dimensions({"visual_structure": 70, "color": 30}),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    # base40 + dim60 - min(100,60) = 40; real photo penalty 0.
    assert result["score"] == 40
    assert result["level"] == "L3"
    assert _step_score(result, "dimensions") == 40.0
    dim_note = next(s["note"] for s in result["steps"] if s["step"] == "dimensions")
    assert "封顶" in dim_note


def test_dimension_deductions_partial() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片"),
        _dimensions({"visual_structure": 5, "color": 3}),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    # 100 - 8 = 92 → L1.
    assert result["score"] == 92
    assert result["level"] == "L1"


# --- High-score veto: score>=80 with hard_defects → capped to 79 → L2. ---


def test_high_score_veto_caps_to_79_and_downgrades_to_l2() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片", hard_defects=["构图敷衍"]),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 79
    assert result["level"] == "L2"
    # raw_level (pre-veto) reflects the un-capped 100 → L1.
    assert result["raw_level"] == "L1"
    assert any(cap["cap"] == "high_score_veto" for cap in result["caps"])


def test_high_score_veto_not_triggered_without_hard_defects() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片", hard_defects=[]),
        _dimensions(),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 100
    assert result["level"] == "L1"
    assert not any(cap["cap"] == "high_score_veto" for cap in result["caps"])


def test_high_score_veto_not_triggered_below_threshold() -> None:
    # Deduct to 79 (below threshold 80) even with hard_defects present.
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片", hard_defects=["构图敷衍"]),
        _dimensions({"visual_structure": 21}),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 79
    assert not any(cap["cap"] == "high_score_veto" for cap in result["caps"])

# --- score→L boundaries (80/60/40/20), L5 = worst direction. ---


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("deduction", "expected_score", "expected_level"),
    [
        (0, 80, "L1"),   # 80 → L1
        (1, 79, "L2"),   # 79 → L2
        (20, 60, "L2"),  # 60 → L2 boundary
        (21, 59, "L3"),  # 59 → L3
        (40, 40, "L3"),  # 40 → L3 boundary
        (41, 39, "L4"),  # 39 → L4
        (60, 20, "L4"),  # 20 → L4 boundary
    ],
)
def test_score_to_level_boundaries(deduction, expected_score, expected_level) -> None:
    # Use class_two (base20+dim60=80, cap80) so the whole 80..20 range is reachable.
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="实景照片"),
        _dimensions({"d": deduction}),
        track_key="class_two",
    )
    assert result["score"] == expected_score
    assert result["level"] == expected_level


def test_score_below_20_maps_to_l5_without_redline() -> None:
    # Custom track that can reach a sub-20 score via base_score alone.
    contract = _contract()
    contract["track_classification"]["tracks"].append({
        "key": "class_low",
        "label": "低分档",
        "base_score": 10,
        "dimension_max": 30,
        "track_cap": 40,
        "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "space_aesthetic", "version": "1.3.0"},
    })
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract,
        _precheck(trait="实景照片"),
        _dimensions({"d": 30}),  # 10+30-30 = 10
        track_key="class_low",
    )
    assert result["score"] == 10
    assert result["level"] == "L5"
    # This L5 is score-driven, not a redline hard reject.
    assert result["hard_reject"] is False

    assert excinfo.value.code == "precheck_invalid"


def test_invalid_level_thresholds_override_fails_closed() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["level_thresholds"] = [{"min_score": 50, "level": "L1"}]  # no 0 catch-all
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationAggregatorError) as excinfo:
        aggregate_category_evaluation(
            contract, _precheck(), _dimensions(), track_key="class_one"
        )
    assert excinfo.value.code == "contract.level_scale.catch_all_missing"


# --- Contract-overridable level thresholds. ---


def test_contract_level_thresholds_override_is_honored() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    # Make L1 require >=95 instead of >=80; 一类 AI图 = 85 now falls to L2.
    contract["level_thresholds"] = [
        {"min_score": 95, "level": "L1"},
        {"min_score": 60, "level": "L2"},
        {"min_score": 0, "level": "L5"},
    ]
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, _precheck(trait="AI图"), _dimensions(), track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert result["score"] == 85
    assert result["level"] == "L2"


# --- Output tagging + JSON-serializability + determinism. ---


def test_output_is_tagged_with_versions() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck(), _dimensions(), track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert result["aggregator_version"] == AGGREGATOR_VERSION
    assert result["level_semantics_version"] == LEVEL_SEMANTICS_VERSION == "doc-l5-worst-v1"


def test_output_is_json_serializable() -> None:
    import json

    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(),
        _precheck(trait="AI图", hard_defects=["构图敷衍"]),
        _dimensions({"visual_structure": 5}, evidence={"note": "demo"}),
        track_key="class_one",
    )
    assert json.loads(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)) == result


def test_determinism_same_input_same_output() -> None:
    contract, precheck, dims = _contract(), _precheck(trait="AI图"), _dimensions({"d": 5})
    first = aggregate_category_evaluation(contract, precheck, dims, track_key="class_one")
    second = aggregate_category_evaluation(contract, precheck, dims, track_key="class_one")
    third = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck(trait="AI图"), _dimensions({"d": 5}), track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert first == second == third

===== FILE backend/tests/test_category_evaluation_contract.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.category_evaluation_contract import (
    CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
    CategoryEvaluationContractError,
    canonical_contract_hash,
    validate_category_evaluation_contract,
)
from app.redline_policy import REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION


def _four_level_scale() -> dict:
    return {
        "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
        "levels": [
            {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
            {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
            {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
            {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
            {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
        ],
    }


def _redline_policy() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION,
        "enabled": True,
        "hit_level": "L5",
        "hit_score_cap": 49,
        "rules": [
            {
                "key": "screenshot",
                "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                "match_any": ["是截图"],
                "exemptions": [],
            }
        ],
    }


def test_contract_accepts_l1_to_l4_scale_when_redline_uses_l4() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["level_scale"] = _four_level_scale()
    contract["redline_policy"]["hit_level"] = "L4"
    validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)


def test_contract_rejects_redline_level_disabled_by_category() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["level_scale"] = _four_level_scale()
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "level_scale.redline_level_disabled"


# Inspiration-image three tracks: 一类 40+60=100, 二类 20+60=80, 三类 40+30=70.
def _track_classification() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
        "tracks": [
            {
                "key": "class_one",
                "label": "一类",
                "base_score": 40,
                "dimension_max": 60,
                "track_cap": 100,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {
                    "schema_key": "space_aesthetic",
                    "version": "1.3.0",
                },
            },
            {
                "key": "class_two",
                "label": "二类",
                "base_score": 20,
                "dimension_max": 60,
                "track_cap": 80,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {
                    "schema_key": "space_aesthetic",
                    "version": "1.3.0",
                },
            },
            {
                "key": "class_three",
                "label": "三类",
                "base_score": 40,
                "dimension_max": 30,
                "track_cap": 70,
                "dimension_schema_ref": {
                    "schema_key": "space_aesthetic",
                    "version": "1.3.0",
                },
            },
        ],
        "default_track": "class_three",
    }


def _common_modifiers() -> dict:
    return {
        "format_version": "common-modifiers-v1",
        "media_type_penalty": {
            "baseline": "real_photo",
            "penalties": {
                "real_photo": 0,
                "render_3d": -5,
                "ai_image": -15,
                "other": 0,
            },
        },
        "high_score_veto": {"threshold": 80, "cap_to": 79},
    }


def _contract() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        "redline_policy": _redline_policy(),
        "track_classification": _track_classification(),
        "common_modifiers": _common_modifiers(),
    }


def test_valid_v3_contract_passes() -> None:
    assert validate_category_evaluation_contract(_contract()) is None


def test_inspiration_track_numbers_are_the_expected_boundaries() -> None:
    tracks = {t["key"]: t for t in _track_classification()["tracks"]}
    assert (tracks["class_one"]["base_score"], tracks["class_one"]["dimension_max"], tracks["class_one"]["track_cap"]) == (40, 60, 100)
    assert (tracks["class_two"]["base_score"], tracks["class_two"]["dimension_max"], tracks["class_two"]["track_cap"]) == (20, 60, 80)
    assert (tracks["class_three"]["base_score"], tracks["class_three"]["dimension_max"], tracks["class_three"]["track_cap"]) == (40, 30, 70)


def test_bad_schema_version_fails_closed() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["schema_version"] = "evaluation-category-profile-v2"
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "schema_version_unsupported"


def test_missing_block_fails_closed() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    del contract["common_modifiers"]
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "block_missing"


def test_redline_block_errors_are_wrapped_with_prefixed_code() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["redline_policy"]["hit_level"] = "L9"
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "redline_policy.hit_level_invalid"


def test_track_cap_boundary_base_plus_dim_equals_cap_is_valid() -> None:
    # 一类 40+60=100 sits exactly on base+dim<=cap<=100; must pass.
    validate_category_evaluation_contract(_contract())


def test_track_cap_violation_fails_closed() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    # base_score+dimension_max (40+60=100) now exceeds track_cap 70.
    contract["track_classification"]["tracks"][0]["track_cap"] = 70
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "track_cap_inconsistent"

        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)

    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError) as excinfo:
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)
